### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/DexLink_Synthesizing_Dexterous_Behaviors_from_Chained_Hand-object_Poses|DexLink: Synthesizing Dexterous Behaviors from Chained Hand-object Poses]]

![[assets/2611.34936_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34936](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34936)

- **作者**: Meenal Parakh, Deep Patel, Beining Han, Jia Deng

#### 研究背景与动机

《DexLink: Synthesizing Dexterous Behaviors from Chained Hand-object Poses》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Synthesizing diverse dexterous manipulation policies is challenging: existing simulation-based approaches rely on human demonstrations which are difficult to scale, or use generic reward functions, which provide limited control over the hand-object interactions. We propose DexLink, which addresses these limitations by synthesizing diverse dexterous behaviors from sparse, reusable hand-object interaction templates. Given key task waypoints, DexLink retargets static interaction templates to target objects using an optimization that preserves spatial hand-object relationships. It then links the retargeted poses into an approximate reference trajectory and uses residual reinforcement learning to refine this reference into a closed-loop policy in simulation. Experiments spanning single-hand, bimanual, articulated, and dynamic tasks demonstrate that DexLink provides fine-grained control over task timing, grasp types, and high-level execution. The modular interaction templates transfer reliably across object scales, instances, and categories; without object-specific human demonstrations, the framework also matches the performance of dense reference-tracking methods. Finally, zero-shot deployment of policies trained purely in simulation shows promise for the simulation-based framework.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Cairn_Object-Centric_3D_Trajectory_Priors_for_Steering_Frozen_VLAs|Cairn: Object-Centric 3D Trajectory Priors for Steering Frozen VLAs]]

![[assets/2611.34914_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34914](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34914)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34914
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Cairn_Object-Centric_3D_Trajectory_Priors_for_Steering_Frozen_VLAs|Cairn: Object-Centric 3D Trajectory Priors for Steering Frozen VLAs]]
- **作者**: Agelos Kratimenos, Kostas Daniilidis, Evangelos Chatzipantazis
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 1.4（加权：具身智能 0.9，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, ComputerVision

#### 研究背景与动机

《Cairn: Object-Centric 3D Trajectory Priors for Steering Frozen VLAs》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are capable low-level controllers, but they tie where objects go to the layouts they were trained on: when objects are moved or obstacles block the way, they carry them to the wrong place or into collisions. Fine-tuning on new layouts needs robot data for each one and erodes skills the model already has. We introduce Cairn, which keeps the VLA frozen and steers it at test time along learned 3D trajectories of the object in its gripper. A small prior reads point clouds of the scene and the held object, along with its pose when grasped and the instruction, and samples SE(3) trajectories up to its release that lead it around obstacles and turn it to fit through gaps. The prior and the VLA form the two levels of a hierarchical planner in which feedback runs both ways: the VLA's intended motion chooses and reshapes the trajectories, and the chosen one guides the VLA's denoising. Its predicted actions, mapped to the held object's poses through the grasp, are pulled toward the next waypoint, while the VLA's weights stay untouched. Across 1,941 held-out LIBERO scenes with displaced objects and obstacles, Cairn raises the success rate of a frozen π0.5 from 24.5% to 95.1%, above that of a π0.5 fine-tuned on our training layouts, without hurting standard LIBERO. On the third-party LIBERO-PRO benchmark, it raises success from 27.3% to 38.0%, and from 54.4% to 81.1% on the episodes it steers. On a real Franka, with a prior trained purely in simulation, it guides the frozen DROID π0.5 around an obstacle and to a placement at the limit of the arm's reach, where it otherwise fails. All code, models and data will be made public.

</details>

- **作者**: Yujia Zeng, Yuxin Chen, Yuzhen Song, Runhan Huang, Chensheng Peng, Yiyang Shao, Koushil Sreenath, Masayoshi Tomizuka, Yilun Du
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《FineDex: Bridging Fine-Grained Human-Object Interaction Generation to Dexterous Humanoid Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FingerBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Dexterity is, at its core, finger-level control, yet existing dexterous manipulation and human-object interaction (HOI) generation methods rarely let one specify how an interaction is performed: five-fingered hands are often reduced to grippers, or finger usage is copied from individual human demonstrations. We present FineDex, a framework that makes finger-level intent both expressible and executable. FineDex learns coordinated human body, hand, and object motions that follow fine-grained instructions on hand selection, individual fingers, and contacts with articulated objects. To bridge generation and execution, FineDex leverages generated interactions as actionable priors for an agent that discovers physically feasible contact strategies through practice in a physics-based digital twin. By adapting at test time without per-instance retraining, FineDex generalizes across fine-grained instructions and object configurations. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art HOI generation quality and fine-grained controllability, substantial improvements in dexterous manipulation success, and successful deployment on a real dexterous humanoid robot.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/RePace_Reasoning_about_Demonstration_Speedup_for_Faster_Vision-Language-Action_Policies|RePace: Reasoning about Demonstration Speedup for Faster Vision-Language-Action Policies]]

![[assets/2611.34864_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34864](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34864)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34864
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/RePace_Reasoning_about_Demonstration_Speedup_for_Faster_Vision-Language-Action_Policies|RePace: Reasoning about Demonstration Speedup for Faster Vision-Language-Action Policies]]
- **作者**: Insup Lee, Hojin Jeon, Sunguk Shin, Seoungbin Bae, Taekwan Kim, Jinwoo Shin, Dongsu Han
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 2.2（加权：具身智能 1.8，大模型 0.1，机器人 0.3）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Multimodal

#### 研究背景与动机

《RePace: Reasoning about Demonstration Speedup for Faster Vision-Language-Action Policies》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World, TempoVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Since vision-language-action (VLA) policies can inherit the slow pace of human demonstrations, accelerating these demonstrations before fine-tuning can lead to faster VLA policies without an additional speed-selection module at deployment. Several offline speedup methods have focused on identifying segments that require less precision and assigning predefined speedup factors to the segments. However, even within a single segment, a speedup factor suitable for steady motion may be too high when precise alignment is required. To this end, this paper presents RePace, an offline reasoning framework that determines how much to accelerate each part of a demonstration. For the same recorded movement, RePace measures how much each candidate speedup factor shortens the sequence and compares the resulting observation-action pairs used for training. Given the task instruction, a pretrained language model uses the visual context and original motion of successive windows to interpret these candidate retiming effects and select a speedup profile for the complete episode. Experimental results demonstrate that RePace outperforms the baselines in overall task execution efficiency on both LIBERO and RoboCasa-Kitchen. On all three tasks with a gripper-equipped Franka Research 3 arm, RePace matches or exceeds the acceleration baselines in success rate and completes successful trials faster.

</details>

![[assets/2611.34764_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34764](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34764)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34764
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Anytime-Valid_Sequential_Verification_for_Sensing-Efficient_Robot_Navigation_under_Perception_Uncertainty|Anytime-Valid Sequential Verification for Sensing-Efficient Robot Navigation under Perception Uncertainty]]
- **作者**: Saad Korchi, Abderrahim Waga
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.3（加权：具身智能 1.2，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《Anytime-Valid Sequential Verification for Sensing-Efficient Robot Navigation under Perception Uncertainty》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

When a robot navigates an unfamiliar grid, noisy sensor readings can make it hard to tell whether the cells along its next move are free. Additional scans can reduce that uncertainty, but a fixed scan count spends the same sensing effort at every position. In a static-grid simulation, a sequential rule uses a Gaussian likelihood ratio to determine when the cells newly covered by the robot's footprint have enough evidence of free space. On 324 held-out scenarios from 18 public maps, the sequential gate and a four-scan reference chosen on validation data both completed every mission without an observed collision. With their respective validation-selected routing weights, the gate averaged 123.15 scans per episode versus 224.13 for the reference, a reduction of 45.05% (95% map-stratified bootstrap interval: 42.21--48.95%). In a retrospective check on the same scenarios with routing weights held equal, the reductions were 43.91% and 43.37% at the two tested weights, with all missions completed. On 216 scenarios from 12 previously unused maps, allocating more of the same certification error budget near the initial start--goal rectangle cut the sequential gate's scan count by a further 11.58%. The spatial variant completed 206 missions versus 208 for the original gate and increased computation time. The collision bound depends on the assumed observation model and footprint geometry. In a stress test with persistent per-cell bias, the original gate completed 0 of 54 missions.

</details>

- **作者**: Divyansh Pandey, Shubh Garg, Vedant Khandelwal, Niyati Rawal, Amit Sheth
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《How Well Do Vision-Language Scene Graphs Serve as Memory for Embodied Question Answering?》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ScanNet, VSI-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Embodied QA needs memory, but answer accuracy mixes retained evidence, reading errors and question priors. For a simulated multi-room scene-graph pipeline we define answerability: the fraction of questions a fixed rule-based reader answers correctly from the graph alone. The baseline recovers 24.1% of visible object types and its room labels flicker. Four targeted repairs double recall, raise answerability from 27.2% to 37.4%, and improve retrained QA from 71.4% to 75.9%, yet better object F1 can lower answerability. A question-type prior beats this reader on every predicted graph. In one real-video test, graph memory lowers accuracy. These exploratory findings favour scoring retained evidence alongside accuracy.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《ELMO: Emergent Representations In Imitation Learning Via Dynamic Hierarchical Action Chunking》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：H-Net, Real-World, SA-VLA, SmolVLA, VQ-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot trajectories naturally decompose into variable-length phases with distinct semantic intents, such as reaching, grasping, and lifting. However, training policies to minimize MSE treats all actions as equal, obscuring their semantic structure. While latent action models attempt to create a structured prediction target by compressing action chunks into latent representations, existing approaches rely on two-stage training and context-blind decoders. Consequently, their representations are context-agnostic motion codes (e.g., "move down") which fail to capture behavioural intent (e.g., "pick up the block"). To overcome these bottlenecks, we introduce the Emergent Latent-action MOdel (ELMO), an end-to-end framework that encodes robot trajectories as grounded semantic codes -- representations that ground task-level behavioural intent in the current environment. ELMO jointly trains a VLA planner and an observation-conditioned decoder over dynamically chunked trajectories, learning a semantically grounded action space without separate pretraining. Across standard benchmarks, ELMO outperforms comparable prior methods by 9.6 percentage points on LIBERO-Plus and by 0.75 in average task length on CALVIN. Furthermore, on Blocks -- our shortcut-free simulation and real-world generalization benchmark -- ELMO reaches a 38.6% success rate while all baselines remain below 5%, demonstrating its ability to capture the semantic structure of demonstrations. Website: https://uoft-isl.github.io/elmo.github.io/

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/A_Failure-Driven_Data_Flywheel_for_Post-Training_Vision-Language-Action_Models|A Failure-Driven Data Flywheel for Post-Training Vision-Language-Action Models]]

![[assets/2611.34698_figure.png|800]]

- **cs 子类**: cs.AI

- **相关领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《A Failure-Driven Data Flywheel for Post-Training Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AutoVLA, FIRE-VLA, TakeVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models rarely satisfy all behavioral requirements of a new platform, and teams without a large deployed fleet lack the data to address the resulting failures. We propose a failure-driven data flywheel for post-training such models. It constructs target scenarios absent from the available data by inserting task-relevant entities into reconstructed real-world scenes, and evaluates the model against each behavioral requirement of the target task. Failure rates form a failure map across task conditions and difficulty levels, which is re-evaluated after each round and assigns larger data generation quotas to conditions where the model still fails. We instantiate the framework in autonomous driving for occluded-pedestrian emergence and yielding at stop-sign-controlled intersections, and evaluate it on two VLA models and one non-VLA planner in simulation and on a real vehicle. For occluded-pedestrian emergence, five rounds of post-training improve the critical-phase collision-free rate on the sealed test set from 0.261 to 0.579 for AutoVLA and from 0.390 to 0.765 for ReCogDrive, while the unnecessary braking rate in paired control scenarios never exceeds 0.013. On a full-scale vehicle without real-world training, post-trained ReCogDrive achieves 4 of 5 safe passages, versus 1 of 5 for the base model. Fine-grained evaluation reveals a pattern obscured by the collision-free rate alone: both VLA models learn anticipatory deceleration before reaching the occluding vehicle, while braking after pedestrian emergence remains limited. For stop-sign yielding, the stop-before-line rate rises from 0.100 to 0.897 for AutoVLA and from 0.000 to 0.667 for ReCogDrive. With sequential training from the pedestrian-capability models, it reaches 0.599 and 0.358, respectively, while the pedestrian collision-free rate decreases by at most 0.032.

</details>

![[assets/2611.34655_figure.png|800]]

- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Nav4Manip_From_Navigation_to_Dexterous_Mobile_Manipulation_through_Co-Evolution|Nav4Manip: From Navigation to Dexterous Mobile Manipulation through Co-Evolution]]
- **作者**: Yusen Feng, Zixi Kang, Zhijun Yin, Changxiao Liu, Tianhong Gao, Jiazhao Zhang, Jiahang Liu, Libin Liu, Zhizheng Zhang, He Wang
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《Nav4Manip: From Navigation to Dexterous Mobile Manipulation through Co-Evolution》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：BaSeNet, EQA, InternVLA, OmniVLA, Real-World, Real-to-Sim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Dexterous mobile manipulation is essential for robots to perform useful tasks in complex, unstructured environments. However, existing systems face two challenges. First, navigation remains too coarse for downstream manipulation, which often requires a precise base position and approach orientation rather than merely reaching a meter-scale goal region. Second, navigation and manipulation are commonly trained in isolation, lacking a feedback loop that enables the two capabilities to co-evolve. These limitations prevent current systems from reliably executing long-horizon dexterous mobile-manipulation tasks in real-world environments. To address them, we introduce Nav4Manip, a bidirectionally coupled co-evolution system for long-horizon dexterous mobile manipulation. It comprises two complementary architectures: Nav4Manip-N learns long-horizon navigation and refines its policy through manipulation feedback, while Nav4Manip-M trains a world-action model (WAM) for downstream dexterous manipulation. We train Nav4Manip-N in three stages: multi-task co-tuning learns navigation behavior and manipulation-aware affordance prediction; rejection sampling fine-tuning (RFT) rejects low-quality policy-generated paths and fine-tunes the model on retained trajectories; and reinforcement learning from AI feedback (RLAIF) uses counterfactual preferences to further improve navigation decisions. To support this framework, we establish the Nav-N-Manip benchmark and construct Nav4Manip-Pairs from benchmark rollouts, providing large-scale paired navigation--manipulation trajectories and affordance supervision. Extensive experiments on public vision-language navigation (VLN) benchmarks and Nav-N-Manip demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, while real-world evaluations show that Nav4Manip completes diverse dexterous mobile-manipulation tasks. Project website: https://nav4manip.github.io/.

</details>

- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《Motion Beyond Pixels: Learning Future Visual Representations for Object-Centric Dynamics》归入 具身智能、世界模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、世界模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：WorldFlowBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Many embodied world models predict future states in latent spaces optimized for RGB reconstruction, which do not explicitly prioritize object-level semantics and geometry relevant to motion generation. We present MotionRep, a visual representation world model that jointly generates future DINO representations and 3D object flow, coupling semantic and geometric information with object-centric dynamics. To make high-dimensional DINO features amenable to generative modeling, we introduce DINO-VAE, which combines temporal and channel compression with latent regularization to produce compact latents while preserving semantic and geometric information. Conditioned on an initial observation, object query points, and a language instruction, a unified diffusion transformer predicts future visual representations and 3D object flow through joint attention. MotionRep employs asymmetric modality-specific noise schedules to keep the visual stream ahead of 3D object flow during joint denoising, allowing progressively refined future visual representations to guide flow generation. Experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that MotionRep outperforms pixel-based baselines in 3D object flow accuracy and generalization to unseen scenes, while requiring only three denoising steps for inference. These results support predictive visual representations as an effective future-state space for object-centric dynamics modeling.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Commit2Drive_Perception-Grounded_Language_Decisions_as_Commitments_for_Trajectory_Planning_in_Vision-Language-Action_Driving_Models|Commit2Drive: Perception-Grounded Language Decisions as Commitments for Trajectory Planning in Vision-Language-Action Driving Models]]

![[assets/2611.34614_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34614](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34614)

- **作者**: Zikai Zhang, Hubert P. Shum, Toby Breckon
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV

- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Commit2Drive: Perception-Grounded Language Decisions as Commitments for Trajectory Planning in Vision-Language-Action Driving Models》归入 具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AutoVLA, CF-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models introduce language reasoning into end-to-end autonomous driving. They output a language decision together with a trajectory, which makes the planner more interpretable. However, in existing methods the language decision is either an independent output or an index that selects among similar candidate trajectories, and its annotations are not verified against the trajectory. As a result, the language decision and the trajectory are often inconsistent in closed-loop driving. To address this, we propose Commit2Drive, a framework that imposes the language decision as a consistency constraint on trajectory planning. The decision is a commitment, a maneuver class with a continuous parameter that one deterministic rule reads from any trajectory, and it is grounded in facts reported by the perception of the model itself. A Maneuver Bank maps the commitment to a code with a kinematic meaning, and consistency losses train the planner to execute every admissible decision of a scene, so that the trajectory follows the decision. An automatic data pipeline supplies the supervision. Decisions are labeled from the future motion of the expert, counterfactual executions train the decision and the trajectory together, and rule-based functions with a Vision-Language Model (VLM) generate, review and refine the language annotations. The code is a lookup that involves no hidden state of the language model, so Commit2Drive is extensible to rule-based decisions, which the same planner executes without retraining. Commit2Drive uses a 0.5B language model and runs closed-loop inference online on a single consumer graphics card. On Bench2Drive, Commit2Drive reaches a Driving Score (DS) of 78.50 and a Success Rate (SR) of 55.91% with a lightweight model, which is competitive with the state of the art, and greatly reduces the language-action inconsistency. Code will be released soon.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Geometric_Acceleration_Obstacles_Physical_Boundary_Structure_and_Affine_Safety_Certificates|Geometric Acceleration Obstacles: Physical Boundary Structure and Affine Safety Certificates]]

![[assets/2611.34586_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Xuheng Gao, Yuhan Pang, Bi-Ke Zhu, Ronghao Chen, Lingxiao Meng, Jiankun Wang, Max Q. -H. Meng
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Geometric Acceleration Obstacles: Physical Boundary Structure and Affine Safety Certificates》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Collision-Set。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Safe robot navigation in dynamic environments requires collision avoidance that respects motion constraints while remaining computationally efficient. Conventional velocity-obstacle (VO) methods characterize collision-inducing motions in velocity space. Although some formulations account for acceleration-limited velocity transitions, their collision constraints remain tied to specific velocity-tracking models. Acceleration obstacles (AO) address this limitation by representing collision-inducing inputs directly in acceleration space. However, existing AO methods either rely on conservative approximations or derive analytical structure expressions without verifying their geometric correspondence. To address this gap, this paper presents Geometric Acceleration Obstacles (GAO), which provides, to the best of our knowledge, the first unified and physically validated characterization of AO geometry and its supporting structure. Specifically, we derive configuration-dependent half-planes that globally support the collision set and establish their tightness, thereby separating certified collision-free acceleration inputs from collision-inducing parts. These geometric results yield unified affine safety constraints. Combined with actuator limits, these constraints define a quadratic program (QP) for real-time navigation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GAO achieves competitive success rates and the lowest control effort among the evaluated methods, with millisecond-level computational latency. Further circle-interchange simulations demonstrate scalability to 1,000 agents. Physical experiments validate the approach in pedestrian encounters, reciprocal multi-agent navigation, and high-speed maneuvering.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Riding the Wave: Sim-to-Real Humanoid Control on Elastic Terrain》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Humanoid robots on deformable elastic terrains face a surface whose position and motion are coupled in space and time: deflection and rebound persist between contacts, and load at one foothold alters the support at others, so training must capture how each contact changes the support that follows. We present RIPPLE (Robust Interaction via Proprioceptive Policies on Large-deformation Elastic terrain), a simulation-to-real learning framework for humanoid control on such terrains. RIPPLE integrates ElasticMembrane, a pretensioned mass-spring mesh that models spatially coupled deflection, energy storage, and rebound, together with deformable contact into MJLab for GPU-parallel policy learning. Staged training then transfers rigid-ground skills to balance and jumping on these terrains using only proprioception, without vision or force sensing. Deployed zero-shot, the policies enable a MagicBot Z1 humanoid to balance, jump consecutively, and enter a trampoline from a rigid platform. In simulation, ElasticMembrane training holds standing success of at least 98.7% (mean over three seeds) on ElasticMembrane, IndependentSprings, and BouncyPlate under 0.3 m/s perturbations, whereas rigid-ground or contact-compliance training transfers poorly to the first two. A fixed policy tolerates stiffness from 0.35 to 2.8 times the training value, beds up to 7 m wide, and seven further simulated support scenarios, so transfer needs no calibration to the trampoline's stiffness. We plan to release the code soon. The interactive browser demo, videos, and scene configurations are available on the project page: https://fly-pigTH.github.io/wave_rider/

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/M$^{2}$AdaptVLA_Multimodal_Memory-Augmented_Vision-Language-Action_Model_with_Predictive_Test-Time_Adaptation|M$^{2}$AdaptVLA: Multimodal Memory-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Model with Predictive Test-Time Adaptation]]

![[assets/2611.34560_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34560](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34560)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34560
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/M$^{2}$AdaptVLA_Multimodal_Memory-Augmented_Vision-Language-Action_Model_with_Predictive_Test-Time_Adaptation|M$^{2}$AdaptVLA: Multimodal Memory-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Model with Predictive Test-Time Adaptation]]
- **作者**: Liufan Tan, Tong Wang, Yuehao Yin, Jian Chen, Yihang Yin, Ying Qu, Wenyu Li, Ruyi Gan, Hao Wang
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 2.7（加权：具身智能 1.8，大模型 0.4，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《M$^{2}$AdaptVLA: Multimodal Memory-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Model with Predictive Test-Time Adaptation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AdaptVLA, DAM-VLA, DeltaNet, EventVLA, FM-VLA, M2AdaptVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Effective robotic manipulation calls for multimodal memory and rapid adaptation to shifts in contact dynamics. However, many existing policies, which retain either visual or wrench history, may miss key cues from the other modality. Moreover, even with wrench memory, fixed policies can struggle when contact conditions shift at deployment. To this end, we introduce M$^{2}$AdaptVLA, a vision-language-action model that integrates visual and wrench memories with predictive test-time adaptation. For visual memory, the model uses test-time training (TTT) to encode observation history in fast weights. Each entry in the bounded TTT bank combines the corresponding frame's features with a readout from these weights, preserving cues that predate the oldest retained frame. For wrench memory, a context gate controls how much the action expert draws on wrench history as it jointly denoises future actions and wrenches. For predictive test-time adaptation, the auxiliary wrench prediction loss also provides online self-supervision. Using measured wrenches as targets, we update low-rank policy adapters by backpropagating only through the final denoising step. Real-world experiments show that the TTT bank outperforms visual memory baselines on progress tracking, scene recall, and demonstration following. On contact-sensitive tasks, the context gate yields higher success rates than direct injection or gated cross-attention. Predictive test-time adaptation improves robustness to shifts in contact dynamics. On eight tasks requiring visual and wrench memory, M$^{2}$AdaptVLA achieves a mean success rate of 82.71%, a 31.46% absolute gain over the strongest baseline. The project page is available at https://tlf-tlf.github.io/M2AdaptVLAPage/.

</details>

- **作者**: Yanran Jiang, Cécile Paris, Dana Kulić, Leimin Tian
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Dynamic Task Update Benchmark (DTU-Bench): Evaluating Runtime Task Adaptation in Robotic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DTU-Bench, DynamicVLA, OpenVLA, RynnVLA, SwitchVLA, TIC-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) policies perform well on fixed-instruction robotic manipulation benchmarks, but their ability to adapt to instruction changes during execution remains understudied. We introduce the Dynamic Task Update Benchmark (DTU-Bench), evaluating 40 task revisions across seven update types and three execution stages using LIBERO, a simulated robotic manipulation benchmark. We compare performance when a new instruction is given before execution starts versus during execution, and examine whether the new task instructions are followed. Across 13 representative VLA configurations, average task success is 95.8% on the original object-manipulation tasks, compared with only 1.6% when the instruction changes which object to manipulate during execution. Controlled diagnostics show that policies often complete the original task before the updated one, and that their responses depend on the robot's physical state. We further test whether a simple scripted controller that releases the grasped object and moves the arm back helps robots respond to instruction changes. This improves success from 2% to 38% for π0.5 and from 8% to 48% for DM0.5, but can cause unwanted object motion. These findings reveal limitations that fixed-instruction benchmarks and task-success metrics alone can overlook in evaluating robotic manipulation. Project page: https://dtu-bench.github.io/

</details>

![[assets/2611.34519_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34519](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34519)

- **作者**: Shuai Yuan, Yuwei Gui, Mingshuang Luo, Zhuo Li, Siyuan Meng, Wenhao Cui, RuiBing Hou, Bingpeng Ma
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《AGLR: Attribute-Guided Latent Refinement for Fine-Grained Humanoid Motion Generation and Editing》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Humanoid motion generation must accommodate evolving task requirements, where localized adjustments should satisfy new geometric constraints without disrupting desired motion details. However, language-based control lacks geometric precision, while joint-level constraints require explicit spatial targets. Local geometric constraints are also underdetermined, complicating target satisfaction and source preservation. We present AGLR (Attribute-Guided Latent Refinement), a unified framework for fine-grained humanoid motion generation and editing through source-anchored refinement. A compact geometric interface supports composable, time-varying control of limb extents, torso orientation, and root trajectories. Given a generated or encoded base latent, an Attribute Residual Adapter predicts a single-pass correction, with dual-level supervision enforcing target matching and selective preservation across attributes and motion coordinates. On HumanML3D-G1, AGLR achieves the highest controllable-generation and preservation-constrained editing success rates among evaluated methods. Editing take only 9.1 ms for a cached 500-frame motion, without additional sampling or test-time optimization. Zero-shot evaluation on BONES-SEED-G1 further demonstrates transfer without fine-tuning.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《HoloBrain-0.5 Technical Report》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：LingBot-VLA, OpenVLA, SmolVLA, SpatialVLA, TinyVLA, X-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present HoloBrain-0.5, a family of vision-language-action (VLA) models for manipulation across robot embodiments. The policies plan in a shared action space using robot state, kinematic structure, and calibrated camera observations. We standardize joint axes, coordinate frames, and tool-center points in robot descriptions (URDFs) and encode their kinematic graphs in a shared 3D representation. HoloBrain-0.5-Continuous generates action chunks by diffusion, while HoloBrain-0.5-Discrete uses residual finite scalar quantization (FSQ) and delayed autoregression. We pretrain both policies on over 10,000 hours of real-robot, simulation, and human-manipulation data spanning 15 embodiment categories. Across six closed-loop simulation settings, the discrete policy converges faster and reaches a higher mean success rate than the continuous policy (56.77% vs. 49.71%). On seven real-robot tasks, it achieves a mean progress score of 87.76% without task-specific post-training. After post-training, the continuous policy outperforms $π_{0.5}$ on the seven-task benchmark (94.52% vs. 86.25%) and an 11-task dual-arm benchmark (79.07% vs. 69.41%). The discrete-initialized continuous policy transfers five tasks zero-shot from Piper to Piper-X, scoring 63.28% on average. With target-robot demonstrations, the continuous policy scores 89.2% on Franka Panda and 92.94% on a mobile dual-arm platform. Although pretraining includes no Unitree G1 data, post-training with limited G1 demonstrations yields a mean progress score of 70.24% across three humanoid tasks, compared with 11.00% for $π_{0.5}$.

</details>

![[assets/2611.34422_figure.png|800]]

- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34422

- **作者**: Felix Tristram, Benjamin D. Killeen, Nassir Navab
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO

- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 世界模型, 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《Can Visual World Models See Force?》归入 具身智能、世界模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、世界模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FD-VLA, OpenVLA, TA-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models and vision-language-action policy models are increasingly proposed as foundations for robot manipulation. They predict how scenes evolve and which actions to take, yet successful manipulation depends not solely on following observable trajectories but also on the hidden variable of interaction force. Neurophysiology has shown that humans possess a visual prior for this hidden variable, motivating us to ask the question: Do the representations of visual foundation models also capture the forces involved in an interaction? We define force awareness as the ability of representations to (1) indicate when contact force is applied, (2) track how it changes during an interaction, and (3) reflect how large it is. We probe frozen image, video, world- and policy model representations with a lightweight readout trained on synchronized video and force measurements from two manipulation datasets. Our experiments show that most models capture when contact occurs but not how much force is being applied. Surprisingly, robot post-training does not improve this either, highlighting an important gap in current models capabilities.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robots executing tasks in partially observed environments must resolve uncertainty while operating with incomplete skills. Inspired by human curiosity, we investigate how robots can formulate task-relevant hypotheses, seek evidence, and acquire missing skills during task execution. We propose CHASE, a Curiosity-guided Harness for Acquiring Skills from Evidence, which operationalizes curiosity as selecting investigations that resolve task uncertainty and expand executable capabilities. Four modules connect dynamic scene understanding, hypothesis-driven planning, on-demand skill acquisition, and verified execution. The planner balances information value, skill reuse, and acquisition/execution cost. Missing skills trigger bounded exploration, learning from verified segments, and held-out admission during acquisition; separate checks verify physical effects and acquired evidence. Newly admitted skills expand the executable investigation set, closing the loop between resolving uncertainty and acquiring capabilities. Simulation and real-robot experiments evaluate investigation, skill acquisition, and task execution. CHASE achieves 50-80% task success under combined information and capability gaps, with 70-90% evidence resolution.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Embodied Lower-limb Exoskeleton Assistance: Survey, Challenges, and Perspectives》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Over the past decade, artificial intelligence (AI) has enabled lower-limb exoskeletons to adapt to complex tasks and assist diverse users. Existing reviews, however, are organized around mechanical design, control strategies, or individual AI algorithms, and provide little systematic coverage of embodied lower-limb exoskeletons and their enabling technologies. This review defines embodied lower-limb exoskeletons and organizes the literature around four dimensions: structural self-adaptation, task adaptation, user-specific adaptation, and control self-regulation. In each dimension, state-of-the-art and representative studies are classified and discussed in terms of the associated challenges, including lightweight and compliant design, data acquisition and fusion, optimization of complex policies, and robust, compliant control. The review then identifies the principal limitations of current research and outlines future directions along these dimensions. From an embodied perspective, this review sets out the closed-loop relationship among mechanical structure, sensing, and control as a framework for analyzing lower-limb exoskeleton performance and for research on human-robot interaction. The directions it identifies point toward lightweight, comfortable, and practical physical assistance.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《One Patch, Many Policies: Object-Associated Physical Patch Attacks on Robotic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World Action Models (WAMs) are vulnerable to adversarial visual interference, motivating the study of universal and transferable physical patch attacks. We propose Object-Associated Patch Attack (OAPA), a universal and transferable physical patch framework that enables an attacker to select which task object's manipulation to disrupt through patch placement, without re-optimizing the patch. Our framework combines object-associated placement, differentiable physical compositing, and final action-expert representation divergence. Physical compositing accounts for perspective and occlusion across camera views, while representation divergence guides patch optimization using either relative RMS or cosine distance. Experimental results demonstrate strong attack transferability across task suites and policies, together with the ability to selectively disrupt manipulation of an attacker-chosen task object through patch placement.

</details>

![[assets/2611.34352_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34352](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34352)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34352
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Beyond_Natural-Language_Instructions_Temporal_Logic_as_a_Task_Language_for_VLA_Models_with_Visual-Logical_Fusion|Beyond Natural-Language Instructions: Temporal Logic as a Task Language for VLA Models with Visual-Logical Fusion]]
- **作者**: Zezhi Liu, Jingwen Sun, Hongjin Chen, Shaojie Ren, Peiyan Tu, Xin Jin, Zhibo Chen
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《Beyond Natural-Language Instructions: Temporal Logic as a Task Language for VLA Models with Visual-Logical Fusion》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Logic-VLA, OpenVLA, StarVLA, TL-VLA, UniVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robotic tasks combine object relations, logical dependencies, and temporal requirements, which robot planning and industrial control commonly specify in temporal logic (TL). We introduce TL-VLA, a vision--language--action framework that replaces natural-language task instructions with temporal logic, making this spatiotemporal structure explicit. A typed graph encoder and visual--logical fusion condition continuous actions on the task specification and the current observations. To prepare a general vision--language backbone for this interface, we develop TL-aware robot pretraining with separate semantic and structural readouts: a frozen language teacher aligns task semantics, the structural readout encodes TL syntax to support complex, long-horizon tasks, and autoregressive action prediction connects the graph representation to robot behavior. Downstream policies inherit the pretrained backbone and TL encoder and learn new fusion and action modules. On CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, our TL-aware initializer reaches \textbf{3.800} average chain length and \textbf{62.3\%} five-task success, compared with 3.578 and 54.5\% for a FAST initializer pretrained on the same data and 3.485 average chain length for a Qwen+NL baseline. On LIBERO-10, TL-aware initialization improves success from \textbf{91.2\% to 95.8\%}. On LIBERO-Mem, our policy reaches \textbf{65.0\%} success without observation history, compared with 45.0\% for a Qwen+NL reference. On held-out Panda planning specifications, it reaches \textbf{59.6\%} at Success@256 over three generation seeds, versus 48.4\% for Qwen+TL and 37.0\% for Qwen+NL. These results support explicit logical task conditioning and transferable TL-aware representations for robot action learning.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《AIRIS: Benchmarking Social Embodied Intelligence in Egocentric Streaming Videos》归入 具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ESTP-Bench, GRASP-Bench, IPIBench, OVO-Bench, ProactiveVideoQA, RTV-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Embodied agents operating around people must not only recognize social cues, but also decide when the available evidence is sufficient, whether to intervene, and how to respond without violating social norms. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these abilities in isolation or with access to completed videos, obscuring where an online social decision fails. We introduce AIRIS, a causal streaming benchmark that formulates social embodied intelligence as a time-anchored chain of Attention, Intention, Reaction, and Interaction (A-I-R-I). AIRIS contains 8,000 expert-curated questions grounded in 357 egocentric clips across 11 context categories and 36 capability dimensions. At each query, models receive only the observation prefix available at that time; decision-aware metrics reward calibrated waiting and complete reactions while penalizing premature commitment. Evaluating 45 proprietary and open-source general-purpose, embodied, streaming-video, and omni-modal models reveals fragmented and temporally miscalibrated competence. General-purpose VLMs excel at passive cue grounding and intent recognition, whereas embodied specialists lead on isolated reaction sub-decisions; yet across all families, models frequently guess intents before sufficient evidence emerges and fail to jointly align intervention timing, action domain, and concrete execution within the same episode. AIRIS thus provides a diagnostic foundation for bridging the gap between passive social perception and reliable embodied interaction.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《LUDIUS: A Benchmark for Embodied Social Intelligence in Everyday Human-Robot Interactions》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：InterSocialBench, MMToM-QA, NIABench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Social intelligence in a robot requires interpreting a situation from the perspectives of the people involved and using that understanding to decide when and how to act. We introduce LUDIUS, a benchmark for evaluating how vision-language models exercise this judgment through a robot's verbal responses and actions, organized around 15 social intelligence skills drawn from social psychology, sociology, and cognitive science. LUDIUS comprises six editions, each a collection of episodes spanning one simulated week. Three editions each cover all 15 skills in a different household. The other three focus on interactions involving multiple people, safety, and context-specific norms, respectively. We evaluate frontier models and a smaller model finetuned on social-interaction data we generated, using a common evaluation harness. The harness supplies each model with the robot's camera views and residents' reactions, retrieves relevant interaction history, and converts the model's tool calls into robot verbal responses and actions. Models tend to perform worse when they must reason about what a resident saw or heard earlier, reconcile competing interests, or fulfill a request within safety constraints and household rules. Beyond the home setting, LUDIUS's design pipeline offers a way to construct social intelligence benchmarks by translating social intelligence skills into context-specific judgments and observable robot behavior.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/BOT-Scale_A_Traceable_Evaluation_Methodology_for_Robotic_Manipulation|BOT-Scale: A Traceable Evaluation Methodology for Robotic Manipulation]]

![[assets/2611.34297_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34297](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34297)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34297
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/BOT-Scale_A_Traceable_Evaluation_Methodology_for_Robotic_Manipulation|BOT-Scale: A Traceable Evaluation Methodology for Robotic Manipulation]]
- **作者**: Yunfei Xue, Kemi Chen, Weiling Chen, Wei Gao, Tiesong Zhao, Zheng-Jun Zha
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.1（加权：具身智能 1.2，机器人 0.9）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《BOT-Scale: A Traceable Evaluation Methodology for Robotic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AutoEval, HealthBench, RoboEval。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A successful manipulation rollout can still involve a mid-task object drop or a placement deviation. Existing evaluators reduce such deviations to a single score or attach explanations computed separately; the score itself explains neither its basis nor what to fix. We show that a manipulation score becomes traceable when defined as the sum of outcome-conditioned penalties over retained behavioral-factor records. Explanation and score share the same computation, so each factor-level deduction names an inspectable deviation. BOT-Scale applies this principle by adapting behavioral assessment scales from medicine. It organizes twelve candidate factors into four assessment layers; each factor is tied to observable evidence and an explicit decision criterion. Reference calibration fixes a penalty per factor, and a closed-form rule aggregates the factor records into a score with per-factor contributions. We developed BOTScorer, an automatic scoring system based on the seven factors retained by data-driven screening, to validate this methodology. We provide BOT-Events, a dataset with eight-rater quality scores and factor-event annotations, alongside the traceable, interpretable score. The code will be released publicly.

</details>

- **作者**: Sergei Kurchev, Iaroslav Kolomiets, Filipp Maksimov, Elizaveta Semenyakina, Miguel Altamirano Cabrera, Artem Lykov, Dzmitry Tsetserukou

#### 研究背景与动机

《DataForgeVLA: Fully Synthetic Manipulation Demonstrations for Vision-Language-Action Training》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DataForgeVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present a dataset of 340 fully synthetic manipulation demonstrations generated from real seed data for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) training. The corpus contains 32,340 frames with synchronized RGB and actions across eight object categories, represented as LeRobot v2.1 episodes with H.264 video, per-frame robot states and pose-valued action labels, language task annotations, summary statistics and quality decisions. Each synthetic demonstration is produced without additional teleoperation: a LoRA-fine-tuned Cosmos-3 model generates a manipulation video, and RoboHaMeR recovers the corresponding robot trajectory from RGB. Quality screening accepted 300 of 340 episodes for policy fine-tuning. As preliminary evidence of feasibility, a pretrained GR00T N1.6 policy fine-tuned exclusively on the 300 accepted episodes reaches 16/20 (80%) real-world pick-and-place success on the Oreo-package task. A policy fine-tuned on 105 real teleoperated episodes achieved 18/20 (90%) under the same protocol. These results are based on one object and 20 trials per policy and are therefore reported as preliminary evidence rather than a general performance claim.

</details>

- **作者**: Xuetao Li, Chen Liu, Jing Wu, Sheng Liu, Miao Li
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《Escapingthe instruction blackhole of vision-language-action policies via optimal-transport wormholes》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA, SwitchVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) policies are trained to follow a fixed instruction, yet people change their minds while the robot acts. We identify the instruction black hole: once a VLA policy commits to a task, new instructions are absorbed and the robot completes the old task or freezes. We quantify it with a transmission ratio and an event horizon and prove it is a blind spot of static imitation, which cannot distinguish a policy that listens from a deaf one once the execution state reveals the task. We propose Wormhole, a training-free plug-in that relocates the robot instead of re-instructing it: optimal transport selects an approach-phase state of the new task, a rate-limited traversal carries the robot there, and the unmodified policy takes over. On LIBERO,Wormhole raises committed-switch success of pi to and pi0.5 from at most 1.2% to 93.1%, matching or exceeding scripted retreat-and-restart with one fifth of its action discontinuity.

</details>

- **作者**: Haoming Gu, Yunpeng Qing, Zhi Hou, Weinan Zhang

#### 研究背景与动机

《CamAct: Learning Robotic Manipulation from In-the-Wild Videos via Camera Motion》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA, Qwen-VLA, Real-World, StarVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models benefit from large, diverse datasets for robotic manipulation, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains costly. Recent work has explored egocentric videos as a source of supervision, primarily drawing on demonstrations of human-object interaction. We show that in-the-wild videos can improve robotic manipulation even when they contain no demonstrations of object manipulation. Specifically, camera motion recovered from these videos provides an additional source of motion supervision that complements robot demonstrations. Building on this finding, we introduce CamAct, which incorporates this camera-motion supervision into VLA policies alongside robot demonstrations, extending the use of human videos for manipulation learning beyond object interactions. Experiments show that incorporating this video supervision during fine-tuning improves success rates from 96.40% to 98.10% on LIBERO and from 22.96% to 29.32% on RoboCasa 365. The resulting policy also demonstrates strong results in out-of-distribution generalization on LIBERO-Plus and real-world manipulation tasks.

</details>

![[assets/2611.34199_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Gen Li, Fan Luo, Mengjun Lin, Shu Han, Bohan Li, Hao Zhao

#### 研究背景与动机

《Loop4D: Embodied World Modeling with 4D VAEs and Looped Diffusion Transformers》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：PointWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Predicting the outcomes of robotic manipulation requires modeling both visual appearance and evolving 3D geometry. This poses two challenges: video compression can obscure fine-grained robot motion, while jointly predicting RGB and geometry demands sufficient generative capacity. We introduce Loop4D, a framework that addresses these challenges through motion-preserving 4D representations and recurrent generative refinement. Our 4D VAE encodes RGB and pointmap sequences into compact spatiotemporal latents while preserving motion details and geometric structure. Our RGB VAE combines geometry-guided supervision with predictive reconstruction to reduce compression-induced motion artifacts. Our geometry VAE uses lightweight adapters to adapt pretrained RGB representations while preserving their priors. Building on these representations, our Dual-Stream Looped Diffusion Transformer maintains separate appearance and geometry streams with shared Transformer weights and cross-modal connections. By reusing Transformer blocks across loops, we increase effective depth without replicating backbone parameters, enabling models with fewer parameters to improve joint RGB-geometry prediction through additional computation. Experiments demonstrate high-quality 4D generation and validate Loop4D in simulated robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>

![[assets/2611.34124_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Ziyu Shan, Xiao Ma, Kangrui Yu, Changjie Wang, Gaoyuan Wu, Zaijia Yang, Chuanrui Zhang, Qirui Li, Yanbo Zhang, Duomin Wang, Ziwei Wang
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《WAtcher: Watching Ahead for Safe Robotic Manipulation with Efficient World-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CoT-VLA, DreamVLA, Real-World, SafeVLA, VideoVLA, WorldVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World-action models (WAMs) couple future imagination with action generation, but they execute every action chunk while ignoring the potential risk. To fill this gap, we present WAtcher, a safety-aware world-action framework in which the policy imagines the outcome of the candidate chunk before execution. For fast future preview, WAtcher builds on a compact WAM whose video expert imagines the visual outcome in only two denoising steps. In addition, we propose Safe-Guard, a lightweight module kept outside the WAM that scores each candidate chunk from its imagined outcome and triggers a bounded retreat when the risk is high. Safe-Guard is detachable and can be applied to other WAMs. To train Safe-Guard, we build an autonomous pipeline to collect hazard data from RoboTwin, LIBERO, and a real robot. Specifically, the pipeline perturbs expert demonstrations and labels each candidate chunk from the physical events recorded when it is executed, such as contacts and losses of support. Using the ~25,000 labeled windows from this pipeline, Safe-Guard reaches 92.1% balanced accuracy on a blind set, and cuts hazardous closed-loop trials on training tasks from 27.0% to 10.8% without lowering task success. On a held-out 300-question benchmark, it scores 94.2%, against 72.5% and 63.3% for zero-shot GPT-6-Astra and Claude Opus 5.5. WAtcher also reaches 90.4% and 82.4% average success on RoboTwin 2.0 and ManiSkill3, on par with or above leading WAMs, and 74.0% on five real-world tasks with Safe-Guard on, against 59.5% for $π_{0.5}$. Project page: https://zyshan0929.github.io/WAtcher

</details>

《CoTacWAM: Joint Modeling of Vision and Touch for Robotic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present CoTacWAM, a world-action model that learns shared contact semantics between wrist vision and touch, achieving the best observed performance in our nominal manipulation and missing-input evaluations. The two modalities observe the same local interaction through different signals: wrist images reveal nearby geometry, while touch records physical contact. Our approach connects their common contact meaning while preserving the evidence unique to each modality, giving the policy a joint description of interaction across available input combinations. This representation supports predictive action learning alongside head-view scene context and conditions corrections after an independent detector confirms a failed attempt. Experiments across four real-world tasks demonstrate improved manipulation performance over the evaluated baselines, robustness to missing local observations, and further gains from autonomous retries.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Dynamic object manipulation requires maintaining spatial and temporal alignment with targets that continue to move during inference and execution. Although future prediction can inform action correction, an accurate forecast does not establish whether modifying an existing action would be beneficial. We present Aegis-VLA, a world-model-guided vision-language-action (VLA) framework that generates predictive action corrections and separately determines whether and how strongly to apply them. A Trajectory World Model combines kinematic extrapolation with learned residual dynamics to predict object motion and estimate positional uncertainty. Discrepancy-Guided Action Refinement uses differences between history-only and base-action-conditioned end-effector forecasts to propose bounded arm corrections. A Reliability Gate learns when to admit corrections based on demonstration-relative improvements over base actions, and regulates their application during inference using predictive uncertainty and anticipated object--end-effector proximity. Rejected corrections preserve the corresponding base actions. Aegis-VLA introduces only approximately 2.45M trainable parameters. On the simulated Dynamic Object Manipulation (DOM) benchmark, Aegis-VLA achieves 56.72\% success over 1,800 evaluation trials, exceeding our reproduced DynamicVLA baseline of 45.44\% by 11.28 percentage points.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Embodied-VGGT_Preserving_Reliable_Local_Geometry_for_Long-Sequence_Reconstruction|Embodied-VGGT: Preserving Reliable Local Geometry for Long-Sequence Reconstruction]]

![[assets/2611.33964_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33964](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33964)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33964
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Embodied-VGGT_Preserving_Reliable_Local_Geometry_for_Long-Sequence_Reconstruction|Embodied-VGGT: Preserving Reliable Local Geometry for Long-Sequence Reconstruction]]
- **作者**: Yiheng Han, Ziang Chen, Zhuyong Fu, Yiqing Yin, Nan Ma
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人

- **关联关键词**: Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《Embodied-VGGT: Preserving Reliable Local Geometry for Long-Sequence Reconstruction》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：RPM-Net。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Long sequence reconstruction must correct accumulated drift while preserving locally consistent geometry. In robotics and autonomous driving, embodied acquisition provides recurring platform- environment geometry and repeated observations of scene structure and platform motion. We present Embodied-VGGT, which exploits these cues to establish reliable local relationships and selectively preserve them during global refinement. Memory-Augmented Grounded Chunks (MAGC) builds on Scal3R's global context memory and uses relative scale references derived from predicted ground geometry to regularize test-time memory updates, encouraging cross-chunk consistency while limiting changes to reliable local geometry. Superpoint Alignment (SA) and Cross-Loop Optimization (CL) jointly address the robust establishment and preservation of geometric relationships. SA balances ground and non-ground evidence across spatial regions to estimate inter-chunk translation. CL uses geometric and motion consistency across interleaved windows to select coherent chunk groups, each receiving a shared Sim(3) correction that preserves its internal relative transformations during global optimization. Experiments on five public driving benchmarks demonstrate consistent and substantial improvements in long sequence reconstruction. On KITTI Odometry, Embodied-VGGT achieves a mean absolute trajectory error of 7.30 m and a Chamfer distance of 2.67 m, reducing these errors by 46.8% and 23.3% compared with the state-of-the-art baseline under same evaluation protocol. To facilitate reproducibility, we released our code: https://github.com/Ang-Sheep/Embodied-VGGT.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ETrack_360UAV-TVLA, EVT-Bench, OmTrackVLA, TVLA, TrackVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Conventional embodied UAV tracking couples target observability with heading control because targets may leave the field of view of forward-facing cameras. We investigate a complementary paradigm that uses full-spherical perception to follow designated targets without active heading adjustment. To support this setting, we introduce 360UAV-ETrack, a simulation benchmark comprising 2,652 sequences, over 1.73 million synchronized observations, eight tracking tasks, and 150 held-out closed-loop scenarios. We propose 360UAV-TVLA, a panoramic vision--language--action (VLA) framework integrating persistent visual referring (PVR), compressed temporal memory (CTM), and geometry-aligned trajectory supervision (GTS) for instance identification, motion reasoning, and three-dimensional control. Experiments across two VLA backbones show consistent gains over standard fine-tuning, with normalized average tracking frames increasing from 22.56\% to 35.28\% for $π_0$ and from 25.68\% to 33.64\% for $π_{0.5}$. Our benchmark further reveals a substantial gap between offline imitation accuracy and closed-loop tracking reliability, establishing a challenging testbed for future research https://libingyu01.github.io/360UAV-ETrack_360UAV-TVLA/

</details>

《iMFPO: Improving Mean Flow Policy Optimization with Drift-Aware Velocity Alignment for Embodied Control》归入 具身智能、强化学习、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、强化学习、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Flow-based generative policies offer expressive action modeling for embodied control, yet achieving efficient one-step generation under reward-driven policy optimization remains challenging. In particular, evolving action distributions during online reinforcement learning may introduce inconsistencies between instantaneous-velocity learning and interval-averaged action generation. To address this challenge, we propose iMFPO, a one-step generative policy optimization framework with decoupled velocity learning and drift-aware velocity alignment. A shared interval-conditioned actor supports instantaneous-velocity supervision and interval-averaged action generation. JVP-corrected velocity regression is jointly optimized with reward-driven policy objectives, with action-level guidance from a frozen PPO teacher during training. At inference, iMFPO directly generates actions through interval-averaged velocity prediction, achieving one-step control without iterative flow integration. Across Go2, G1, H1, and Spot, zero-latent evaluation yields mean returns of 35.69, 34.44, 416.23, and 29.86. On Go2, action-generation latency drops from 15.57 ms to 0.41 ms relative to 64-step RFPO. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/iMFPO. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/iMFPO.

</details>

![[assets/2611.33915_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33915](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33915)

- **作者**: Zeyang Guan, Qihao Zhao, Rui Gong, Zhang Haitian, Yalun Dai, Yangyu Huang, Lingao Xiao, Haozhe Du, Xuehui Yu, Ye Lu, Jianjun Gao, Ruoyu Wang...
