#### 研究背景与动机

《SEEK: Self-Evolution through Execution Knowledge for Agentic Robot Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

General-purpose multimodal foundation models enable robots to adapt during deployment from observations and execution feedback, without task-specific demonstrations or training. However, the robot retains what it learns only in the model's working context. Once the task ends, it loses this execution knowledge, such as how to carry a bowl between two grippers. On the next attempt at the task, the robot may take just as long and repeat the same mistakes. To avoid this repetition, robots must retain execution knowledge across attempts. Yet replaying past actions is insufficient, because their effects depend on the current conditions. Procedures that determine actions from the current scene address this, but when they meet conditions that earlier attempts did not cover, revising them requires the measured effects of earlier actions. We therefore propose SEEK, an agentic framework that retains both, enabling robots to improve across attempts while keeping the underlying model fixed. To adapt retained knowledge to the current scene, SEEK adopts a Self-Evolving Programmatic Policy (SEPP) that encodes manipulation as executable procedures and determines each action from current observations. As the robot executes these procedures, SEEK records the measured effects of its actions, including those from failed attempts, in a Persistent Action-Effect Bank (PAEB). Where a retained procedure applies, it acts without a model request; where it meets a condition it does not cover, this evidence guides its revision. On RoboDojo, knowledge retained over ten attempts raises SEEK's average task score from 27.0 to 54.4 and its success rate from 19.7% to 48.0% across all 42 tasks. On 34 of these tasks with GPT-6.2 Astra, SEEK reaches 80.1 after five attempts, against 49.6 for the same model acting directly, and uses 77.6% fewer model requests. https://zeyangguan.github.io/SEEK-Agentic-Robot-Manipulation/

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Integration of Vision-Language-Action Control for Autonomous Forklift Unloading with Limited Real-World Data》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Forklifts play a critical role in warehousing and distribution, but existing automation systems remain limited in handling complex tasks that require sequential decision-making. Recently, vision--language--action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising approach for multi-step decision-making by directly generating control actions from visual observations and language prompts. Although VLA-based control has been extensively researched for manipulators, to the best of our knowledge, its application to forklifts remains unexplored. Therefore, this study demonstrates the integration of VLA control for an autonomous forklift unloading task with limited real-world data. Moreover, training VLA models typically requires large-scale datasets. As there is currently no public dataset for forklift operations, we use a VLA model pretrained primarily on robotic manipulator data and construct a training dataset via simulation, which can be scaled up at low cost. We then adapt the pretrained VLA model to forklift control using 9.38 h of simulation data and 0.8 h of real-world demonstrations, with the latter accounting for approximately 8% of the collected forklift data by duration. The integrated system was validated through 72 real-world trials across four model variants. The results show that the proposed system can accomplish an unloading task even with limited real-world data.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning a Whole-Body Pivot-Walking Gait for Heavy-Box Transport with a Humanoid》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Through pivot walking, humans can transport objects that are too heavy to lift. Previous humanoid studies have relied on planned sequences in which object manipulation and stepping are executed separately and the object is brought to rest between successive rotations. However, few studies have developed a single learned controller for humanoid pivot walking. In this paper, we propose a single reinforcement-learning policy that jointly coordinates stepping, balance control, and object manipulation. The policy is trained using rewards that promote alternating pivot motions and a phase reference that progresses according to the displacement of the object. In simulation, we confirmed that the humanoid can pivot a box. For deployment, we fine-tuned the policy into an object-blind version that follows a periodic clock instead of the progress reference. On the real robot, the deployed policy transported the box forward by more than 1 m in all five trials; each trial ended at a base-tilt safety stop. These results demonstrate that coordinated humanoid pivot walking can be learned with reinforcement learning and transferred to a physical humanoid robot without direct object-state sensing.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/$_widetilde{_varepsilon}$_Does_Initial_Noise_Induce_Directional_Bias_in_Vision-Language-Action_Models|$\widetilde{\varepsilon}$: Does Initial Noise Induce Directional Bias in Vision-Language-Action Models?]]

![[assets/2611.33816_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33816](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33816)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33816
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/$_widetilde{_varepsilon}$_Does_Initial_Noise_Induce_Directional_Bias_in_Vision-Language-Action_Models|$\widetilde{\varepsilon}$: Does Initial Noise Induce Directional Bias in Vision-Language-Action Models?]]
- **作者**: Junmyeong Lee, Dongmin Shin, Wooseok Jeon, JunGyu Lee, Seunghyun Shin, Hae-Gon Jeon
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.0（加权：具身智能 1.5，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《$\widetilde{\varepsilon}$: Does Initial Noise Induce Directional Bias in Vision-Language-Action Models?》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：BehaviorVLA, DSRL, ResVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have widely adopted diffusion-based action generation for continuous robot motions, enabling fine-grained control across diverse tasks. However, recent studies have shown that their performance is highly sensitive to initial noise, with resulting action errors that make recovery difficult during execution. Existing approaches search for favorable noise initializations to improve performance, yet the action expert itself remains sensitive to noise. In this work, we reveal that initial noise induces a consistent directional bias in action space that closely aligns with the noise's temporal mean. We thus introduce a simple yet effective framework that improves robustness to initial noise through interventions during both training and inference. During training, we enforce consistent action predictions from antithetic noise pairs $(ε, -ε)$ and, at inference, remove the noise's temporal mean. Our method substantially reduces action deviation and improves task performance across simulation benchmarks. In real-robot experiments, our method increases the average task success rate of $π_{0.5}$ from 40% to 68%, with more stable and consistent execution.

</details>

![[assets/2611.33810_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Zexi Zhao, Zizhen Liu, Jing Justin Ye, Huawei Li
- **cs 子类**: cs.CR, cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《FlipVLA: Backdoor Attacks on Vision-Language-Action(VLA) Models Only Through Bit Flips》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Cryptography and Security 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FlipVLA, OpenVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate robot actions from visual observations and language instructions in real-world environments, making malicious model tampering a potential threat to physical safety. We study whether object-triggered backdoors can be implanted through a small, fixed set of parameter bit flips without retraining. The central challenge is to make persistent weight modifications disrupt trigger-present behavior while preserving performance on non-trigger inputs. We propose FlipVLA, a two-stage selector-amplifier method that exploits existing visual features within an FC-GELU-FC structure. The selector constructs a trigger-dependent response in a selected channel, using GELU to attenuate background activity. The amplifier modifies a connected downstream weight to convert this response into an action-relevant perturbation. Channel screening and discrete bit search construct both components under a limited modification budget. Experiments on OpenVLA-OFT and GR00T N1.7 across four LIBERO suites achieve attack success rates of 90.22% to 100%, with task success decreasing by at most 1.5 percentage points on clean scenes.

</details>

![[assets/2611.33794_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33794](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33794)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33794
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Keep_or_Roll_Back_Out-of-Loop_Judges_for_Self-Improving_VLA_and_End-to-End_Embodied_Policies|Keep or Roll Back? Out-of-Loop Judges for Self-Improving VLA and End-to-End Embodied Policies]]
- **作者**: Zilin Huang, Zhengyang Wan, Kangan Qian, Zihao Sheng, Sikai Chen
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.7（加权：具身智能 2.4，机器人 0.3）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《Keep or Roll Back? Out-of-Loop Judges for Self-Improving VLA and End-to-End Embodied Policies》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：HIL-SERL, SimpleVLA-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Self-improvement is now a practical recipe for embodied policies: a policy collects experience, is retrained, and is kept when a judge rates it higher. However, the judge is usually trained or chosen inside the same loop, so its evidence is not independent of the policy. We introduce SealedLoop, an evaluation framework that adds a sealed judge: an evaluator outside the loop that the improver cannot see or optimize against. A new policy is kept only when a sequential test on the sealed judge confirms the gain, with a bounded probability of keeping a worse one. Running the same loop under each judge, we separate two sources of a false gain, which we call judge error (the loop's judge is wrong on its own data) and transfer gap (a real gain there does not carry over to unseen conditions). We evaluate on LIBERO manipulation, NAVSIM driving, and a full-scale real vehicle. In manipulation, a success classifier accepted 65 of 196 failures in one generation. In driving, policies that gain on the loop's own frames lose in an unseen city, so even an exact in-loop judge would keep 8/15 of them. When the sealed test decides, a driving loop built to improve ends with a better policy than under its own critic (0.674 against 0.649 PDMS, from 0.638), and on driving pairs with known sealed gain the test accepted 11 of 12 real gains and none of 59 losses. In a real-world field experiment on a published improvement step, the reinforced version drove further into a curve than the version it started from in all 6 blind pairs, though neither completed it. These results show that evidence from inside the loop, however accurate, is not enough to decide whether a policy has improved. SealedLoop offers a foundation for trustworthy self-improvement of embodied policies, in which progress is judged by evidence the improving system cannot influence.

</details>

![[assets/2611.33772_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Zilin Huang, Zhengyang Wan, Zihao Sheng, Boyue Wang, Ruolin Yang, Yuhao Luo, Sikai Chen
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.0（加权：具身智能 1.5，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《Triage Before Fine-Tuning: Feasibility-Gated Selection and Monitored Adaptation of Embodied Foundation Models for a Fixed Physical Platform》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DriveVLA, UniDriveVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Embodied foundation models are increasingly deployed by selection: a practitioner picks a public checkpoint by benchmark rank and fine-tunes it on local data. The platform is fixed before the model is chosen, with little or no local data. We ask what such a checkpoint loses on a fixed platform and which part of the loss local data can buy back. We split the gap by what can repair it. A sensing gap, information the sensors do not capture, cannot be closed with their data; a latency gap, output arriving after the control loop needs it, belongs to the loop, not the weights; only a distribution gap is fine-tuning's to repair. TRIAGE turns this into feasibility-gated selection and monitored adaptation in five stages: gates on the sensor contract and on end-to-end latency that no score can override, probes on ten minutes of local logs, a ranking by target-relevant score among the candidates that pass, and fine-tuning that is monitored and checked in closed loop before release. On driving benchmarks, a deadline below a leading closed-loop model's latency makes it collide on 80 to 90% of routes, and losing field of view moves plans 2.2 to 5.7 times more than losing resolution at equal pixels. Local frames close 75% of a stability gap but raise perturbation sensitivity at small budgets, and picking by score violates a gate in two of four deployment profiles. On a full-scale vehicle, the factory camera failed the sensing gate and an added camera repaired it. The latency gate removed the two highest-scoring candidates, neither of which completed a closed-loop trip. The selected model passed acceptance in 4 of 5 closed-loop trips, and the closed-loop check withheld a fine-tuned model that had improved offline. TRIAGE lets organizations that cannot train foundation models from scratch find out whether a public checkpoint can run on the robot or vehicle they own, and what to fix if not.

</details>

![[assets/2611.33769_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33769](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33769)

- **作者**: Zirui Li, Yujie Yan, Jingxuan Qian, Yangang Ren, Haizhou Yin, Haoran Xu, Chen Lv
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《FailPoint-X: A Policy-Matched, Failure-Anchored Benchmark for Offline VLA Post-Training from Human Takeovers》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FRBench, Kintsugi-VLA, RePO-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Human takeovers offer corrective supervision for vision-language-action (VLA) policies, but their contribution to offline post-training must be separated from that of continued optimization. We introduce FailPoint-X, a benchmark for offline VLA post-training with human takeovers. To represent uncertainty in failure onset, an annotator marks an interval for each failure, and training takeovers start at seeded frames sampled within it. From one fixed $π_{0.5}$ checkpoint, which also initializes post-training, we assemble 629 natural failures across 10 LIBERO-X task families at up to five difficulty levels. All 2,823 takeover attempts are logged; 2,400 are accepted recoveries, of which 2,320 are used for post-training. Evaluation restarts every method from the fixed midpoints of 384 held-out failure intervals. Under matched actor-update counts, we compare behavior cloning of recoveries, value-based offline learning on recoveries and failed rollouts, and continued training on existing demonstrations. Within 1,200 steps on the 240 L1-L3 held-out failures, cloning improves recovery by 29.4 percentage points (pp) over the demonstration-only control; the value-based learner does not establish a further gain (1.5 pp). In exploratory evaluation from L1-L5 nominal starts at the same horizon, cloning loses 30.0 pp of success relative to the base policy; mixing in demonstrations reduces the loss to 16.5 pp, a reduction that shrinks under a stricter, pre-specified horizon. Restorable failure states and a shared evaluator let all methods be compared from identical starts.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/RegHarness_Regulating_What_Code-as-Policy_Agents_Keep_from_Steps_to_Skills|RegHarness: Regulating What Code-as-Policy Agents Keep from Steps to Skills]]

![[assets/2611.33739_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33739](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33739)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33739
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/RegHarness_Regulating_What_Code-as-Policy_Agents_Keep_from_Steps_to_Skills|RegHarness: Regulating What Code-as-Policy Agents Keep from Steps to Skills]]
- **作者**: Shengming Liu, Yunyi Gao, Xinyi Wang, Jianyong Sun, Shuai Wang, Yi Deng, Li Chenye, Mingcai Zhou, Sim Kuan Goh
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 大模型, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.0（加权：具身智能 0.9，大模型 0.6，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Multimodal, Agent

#### 研究背景与动机

《RegHarness: Regulating What Code-as-Policy Agents Keep from Steps to Skills》归入 具身智能、大模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：RURL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Code-as-Policy agents generate, execute, and revise robot programs, but often lack explicit mechanisms to govern what persists across executions: which actions are retained within a trial and which code modifications carry over to subsequent runs. This leaves two critical aspects of agent behavior insufficiently controlled: step-level execution and cross-run adaptation. We present RegHarness, a two-level harness that governs both forms of persistence for the RATs agent team, without relying on a vision-language-action (VLA) policy or demonstrations. Within each trial, run-time governance evaluates each proposed step from a reset scene, replaying the committed steps, while a fixed-rule controller commits the step only when both RATs' verifier and a task-long LLM auditor approve it. Between runs, a coding agent uses logged failures to develop and test revisions to skills, primitives, and rules, which remain disabled by default until explicitly enabled by the authors. Using a single low-cost model across all run-time roles, RegHarness achieves an 87.8% success rate (158/180) on paired LIBERO-PRO trials, compared with 39.4% for RATs, and a 72.0% success rate on MolmoSpaces trials. A $2\times2$ ablation study of RegHarness's two levels shows that its skill refinement alone increases RATs' successful trials from 71 to 135, whereas run-time governance alone achieves 105 and RATs' native stepwise reset-and-replay mode achieves only 46. These findings suggest that, for Code-as-Policy agents operating with low-cost models, task success depends not only on how generated programs are executed, but also on the quality of the reusable skills they invoke and how those skills evolve across runs.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/A_general-purpose_safe_manipulator_planning_framework_for_diverse_environments_without_retraining|A general-purpose safe manipulator planning framework for diverse environments without retraining]]

![[assets/2611.33721_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33721](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33721)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33721
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/A_general-purpose_safe_manipulator_planning_framework_for_diverse_environments_without_retraining|A general-purpose safe manipulator planning framework for diverse environments without retraining]]
- **作者**: Mingkang Long, Rong Su, Luhang Hong, Qingqing Wang, Ruochen Tai
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《A general-purpose safe manipulator planning framework for diverse environments without retraining》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Nowadays, robots need to be retrained whenever their operating environment changes, and this prohibitive training cost keeps them far from daily use. The community is calling for innovations that bestow on a robot the ability to safely grasp different objects under different conditions without excessive training data and computing power. In response to this call, we present a general-purpose manipulator mission-planning framework, allowing a robot to safely perform manipulation tasks, such as grasping objects that fit in its gripper, across different operating environments without retraining its controllers. The framework rests on two innovations: a finite-state abstracted model, bisimilar to the original system dynamics, that describes accessibility of the space in front of the robot under its given arm and gripper, and a block-conservative specification generation method that turns each manipulation task under any specific operational environment into specifications about state accessibility of the abstracted model. A task proven feasible and safe on the abstracted model is guaranteed to be safe and achievable by the robot. Real-world case studies conducted on a Unitree G1 U1 humanoid show that manipulation tasks commonly encountered in daily life, such as grasping an object, pressing an elevator button or picking up a berry, can be handled within this framework, where an abstracted model of the G1 U1 robot is built only once to avoid costly model reconstructions, and different tasks are simply captured by specific block-conservative specifications, achievable by a generic mission planner with vision-based feedback control.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/FreshVLA_Frequency-Regularized_Time_Scale_Hierarchical_Latent_Actions_for_Vision-Language-Action_Models|FreshVLA: Frequency-Regularized Time Scale Hierarchical Latent Actions for Vision-Language-Action Models]]

![[assets/2611.33635_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33635](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33635)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33635
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/FreshVLA_Frequency-Regularized_Time_Scale_Hierarchical_Latent_Actions_for_Vision-Language-Action_Models|FreshVLA: Frequency-Regularized Time Scale Hierarchical Latent Actions for Vision-Language-Action Models]]
- **作者**: Jiaming Liang, Yifeng Zhan, Rongyi He, Mu Li, Jiaqi Fan, Zhiyuan Zhang, Qiwei Liang, Zhengjie Zhang, Boyang Cai, Ningyuan Zhang, Xingjian Jing, Qiang Nie
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.3（加权：具身智能 1.8，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《FreshVLA: Frequency-Regularized Time Scale Hierarchical Latent Actions for Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FreshVLA, OpenVLA, Real-World, UniVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Learning reusable latent actions from action-free videos offers an effective route to improving Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies. However, reconstruction-oriented latent actions do not necessarily benefit policy learning: as they may encode task-irrelevant static appearance with scene identity, or entangle slowly varying task progress with fine-grained execution dynamics in limited discrete code. We propose FreshVLA, a VLA framework built on a frequency-guided, dynamics-sensitive learning paradigm for acquiring temporally hierarchical continuous latent actions. FreshVLA uses a high-pass prior to guide visual transition encoding toward dynamical regions, thereby reducing reliance on static appearance cues. It further organizes latent actions into coarse-grained task progress and fine-grained execution dynamics through cross-timescale sampling and frequency-domain regularization, enhancing their capacity to represent long-horizon behaviors. During policy training, we freeze the pretrained latent action encoder and transfer its representations to robot control through joint flow-matching of latent and robot actions. FreshVLA achieves 98.2% average success on LIBERO and 98.0% on LIBERO-Long, exceeding GR00T-N1.6 on the latter by 3.6%. It also reaches an average sequence length of 4.52 on CALVIN and strong overall success rate of 80.4% in the zero-shot LIBERO-PLUS evaluation. Furthermore, FreshVLA exhibits greater robustness to visual perturbations and a more distinct division of roles between two branches in modeling low- and high-frequency motion, respectively. In 35-step action regression, it achieves a 51.3-percentage-point higher relative error reduction than its hierarchical discrete counterpart. Real-world experiments demonstrate that FreshVLA improves the average success rate over $π_{0.5}$ by 7.5%. The FreshVLA project page is available at https://freshvla.github.io/.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《An Adversarial Multi-Agent Pursuit Game for Locomotor Agility Pretraining》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Agile humanoid locomotion requires controllers that can adapt to rapidly changing motion demands while remaining stable and robust. Recent locomotion controllers are typically designed with either a dense reward that is carefully shaped to encourage steps and penalize falling, or by using a reference tracking reward which teaches the controller to follow predetermined motions. Once a behavior that maximizes the reward is discovered, the controller ceases to learn. While generally stable, reference tracking methods focus on motor control separately from motion generation. Self-play has been proven as an effective technique for superhuman performance on several multiagent games like Go, Starcraft, hide-and-seek, and capture-the-flag. We train neural networks to play the game of multiagent tag in a physics simulator to study if new motion generation and control strategies can emerge through sparse feedback and competitive self-play. We present MJTag, a task suite for role-conditioned locomotion during competitive pursuit-evasion. Our model is a role-conditioned policy for a low-level locomotion controller and optional planar velocity commands. MJTag's league-based play with sparse feedback enables a locomotion controller to adopt new strategies, including dodging, sprinting, and tackling.

</details>

- **作者**: Vanshaj Khattar, Jing Liu, Toshiaki Koike-Akino, Ming Jin, Ye Wang

#### 研究背景与动机

《Best-of-$N$ Test-Time Steering Can Lead to Reward Hacking in Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown promise for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but adapting to changing environmental conditions still remains challenging. Recent test-time steering methods have shown promise in enabling adaptive VLA policies by utilizing additional computation during deployment. In this paper, we consider best-of-$N$ test-time steering for VLA models, where multiple action candidates are sampled from a fixed policy and a verifier is used to select the best one during deployment. We show that increasing $N$ does not always improve performance when the verifier is inaccurate, and can favor candidates whose scores exploit prediction errors, a form of reward hacking. We study this effect on 60 LIBERO-Safety task-level cases using a $π_{0.5}$ policy, with ten repetitions per case. We introduce controlled errors in a MuJoCo trajectory verifier and show that as $N$ increases, selected candidate rewards diverge from the accurate verifier rewards, leading to reward hacking. We also show that verifier calibration and softmax-based candidate selection can mitigate reward hacking in some settings.

</details>

![[assets/2611.33390_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33390](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33390)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33390
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/RoboARC_Physical_Puzzles_for_Robotic_Manipulation_under_Spatial_Constraints|RoboARC: Physical Puzzles for Robotic Manipulation under Spatial Constraints]]
- **作者**: Qineng Wang, Jianwen Lyu, Pingyue Zhang, Reuben Tan, Xiaoyu Huang, Tonghe Zhang, Kangrui Wang, Yelong Shen, Jianfeng Gao, Guanya Shi, Ruohan Zhang, Manling Li
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO

- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《RoboARC: Physical Puzzles for Robotic Manipulation under Spatial Constraints》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：BiGym, OSWorld, Terminal-Bench, VLABench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Many hard manipulation tasks come down to a spatial constraint that only a few motions satisfy, such as passing a part through a gap barely wider than itself or separating two interlocked parts. These motions depend on the exact geometry of each scene, so demonstrations and skills recorded elsewhere rarely transfer. We therefore ask: when manipulation hinges on spatial constraints, can robots reason out the right motions? We introduce RoboARC, a benchmark of 90 tasks in six constraint types over rigid and soft materials, on three robots in MuJoCo. Every task is a frozen scene with a physics-based grader and a verified reference solution that replays bit-identically. Each task comes in a Privileged regime, with the full simulator state and resets, and a Standard regime with real-sensor inputs (camera images and proprioception) and a single attempt without resets. We evaluate seven coding agents and four VLA policies, none trained on these tasks. The strongest agent, GPT-6 Astra in Codex, solves 86% of the tasks in the Privileged regime and 43% in the Standard regime, and the four VLA policies, run zero-shot, solve none of their 18 one-arm puzzles. On 15 puzzles that GPT-6 Astra solved in the Privileged regime and failed in the Standard regime, object poses add 4 points and part geometry 13, but both together add 36, from 24% to 60%. Only 7% of the coding agents' failed runs pass more than half of a task, and 53% of the failed runs in the Standard regime are stuck on a single step. Frontier coding agents already reason out the right motions for many of these tasks once they know where the parts are and what shape they have. From their own sensors, in a single attempt, the strongest solves fewer than half of the tasks. The website can be found at https://roboarc.embodied-first-exam.ai

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Innata-0: A Native Embodied World System with Model-Agent Co-Design》归入 具身智能、大模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：RoboDojo-Sim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

The physical world is open-ended, dynamic, and partially observable. While foundation models have advanced embodied intelligence, standalone models struggle with long-horizon execution, and simply integrating models with agents may lead to inconsistencies between planning and physical control. To support reliable and adaptive behaviors in the physical world, we introduce Innata-0, a native embodied world system. Innata-0 jointly designs an embodied world-action model and an agent to combine their complementary strengths. The model, Innata-0-WAM, adopts a tri-tower Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that integrates understanding, prediction, and action generation. The agent, Innata-0-Agent, coordinates long-horizon execution through memory, verification, and recovery. A co-design protocol is further proposed to align model training with agent execution and leverage validated experiences to support subsequent adaptation and model improvement. Extensive evaluations demonstrate strong embodied capabilities across understanding, world prediction, and robotic manipulation. Innata-0 represents our first step toward physical intelligence, and we see it as the beginning of a much longer journey.

</details>

《vSFT: Learning to Verbally Tune Agents at Test Time》归入 大模型、强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language model agents are increasingly improved via agentic reinforcement learning, which can be on-policy but suffers from expensive rollouts and sparse rewards, limiting its application at test time. In this paper, we present a verbally Supervised Fine-Tuning (vSFT) framework that enables the conjunction of dense feedback in natural language and weight updates at test time. Specifically, our vSFT forms a closed loop between an agent and a supervisor via a hypernetwork, in which the supervisor generates verbal supervision from the agent's failed trajectories, and the hypernetwork converts that supervision into low-rank weight updates to tune the agent. We validate that this test-time in-parameter evolving process outperforms both its in-context counterparts and prevalent reinforcement learning methods. In particular, our vSFT using Qwen3-4B outperforms GRPO and Reflexion by 8.7% and 13.9% in success rate on ALFWorld, and by 4.8% and 18.2% on WebShop, respectively. We believe that learning how to adapt a model, rather than an adapted model, offers a promising path towards more capable agents that improve themselves.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents raise safety risks that are not captured by text-only refusal behavior, especially when tools can trigger high-impact actions. This concern is salient in military settings, where autonomous weapons raise the possibility of delegating force decisions to software agents. In this work, we study whether a drone agent's action boundary is stable under changes to the target descriptor. We evaluate recent and popular LLMs in a simulated perception-action pipeline over real-world drone imagery, where models can localize requested targets and invoke a simulated lethal tool. We find two concerning failure modes. First, some LLM agents invoke the lethal tool for ordinary generic descriptors such as \emph{people}, requiring no jailbreak at all. Second, an agent that initially appears conservative under protected descriptors can become unsafe under a simple semantic target shift.

</details>

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Open-world aerial object search methods often rely on persistent spatial maps, learned world models, or task-specific policy training. To examine how far a minimal, training-free design can go, we introduce SimpleUAV, an agent built around a frozen, off-the-shelf vision-language model (VLM). SimpleUAV separates current-view target assessment from history-conditioned action selection, combining fresh candidate evidence with lightweight search context. Local geometry converts visual selections into bounded straight-line movements, depth-based checks halt blocked segments, and measured execution feedback guides subsequent decisions. This closed loop sustains search without persistent spatial maps or task-specific training. SimpleUAV achieves 41.61% Success Rate (SR) on UAV-ON and 11.98% on AerialDojo-200K, compared with 23.80% and 3.20% for the strongest prior baselines, respectively. With limited benchmark-specific adaptation, the same core agent transfers to aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) on TravelUAV, improving Full SR by at least 19.66% across all three splits under L1 assistance. These results establish a strong baseline for evaluating when additional architectural complexity benefits aerial search.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Constraint-Aware_Test-Time_Scoring_for_LLM_and_VLM_Candidate_Selection|Constraint-Aware Test-Time Scoring for LLM and VLM Candidate Selection]]

![[assets/2611.33161_first_page.png|800]]

- **作者**: Rangarajan Ramesh, Yongchao Huang

#### 研究背景与动机

《Constraint-Aware Test-Time Scoring for LLM and VLM Candidate Selection》归入 大模型、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CWEval, HotpotQA, LiveCodeBench, SafeLIBERO, TPVQA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

When an LLM or VLM produces several candidate answers, picking the best one usually relies on the model's own confidence, a learned judge model, or a domain check. Each has a known weakness: an answer can be fluent but insecure, mathematically plausible but physically infeasible, or rated highly by a judge while quietly breaking a task requirement. We propose a scoring framework that combines a small set of hard checks every answer must pass with a wider set of softer checks whose scores are blended into one number per candidate. This adaptive scoring framework consists of three components: a drafting model that produces the candidates, a configuring model that selects and weights the checks for each task, and scoring models that rate properties no grounded tool can compute. We apply our proposed method to four tasks: writing code that is both correct and secure (CWEval), generating robot manipulation plans (LIBERO with added safety variants), passing competitive-programming hidden tests (LiveCodeBench), and answering questions from retrieved documents (HotpotQA). An ablation study isolates the contribution of each component and finds that the largest gains come from combining hard checks with candidate comparison: on tasks where the first sampled answer is often broken or unsafe, this pairing lifts joint correctness-and-safety by up to +0.21 (retrieval-augmented QA) and code-generation safety by up to +0.28 (CWEval), with the size of the gain tracking how weak the first sample is, falling to near zero or slightly negative where it is already strong. The per-task layer that selects and weights the softer checks adds no measurable value over simpler alternatives. This builds a unified scoring framework, together with an ablation that identifies the grounded hard checks, not the soft-factor configuration, as the source of the gain.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted infinite-horizon Markov Decision Processes with recursive entropic risk measures (ERM), parameterized by $β\neq 0$. We consider both risk-averse ($β&gt;0$) and risk-seeking ($β&lt;0$) settings. We consider the offline RL setting, where the agent is provided with a fixed dataset and wishes to learn a near-optimal policy using this dataset. We introduce a model-based algorithm, called ERM Value Iteration Lower Confidence Bound (ERM-VI-LCB) and derive a PAC-type bound on its sample complexity. We also establish a corresponding lower bound, demonstrating that exponential dependence on $|β|/(1-γ)$ asserted by the upper bound is unavoidable in the worst case. The presented bounds provide the first rigorous sample complexity guarantees for offline RL under recursive ERM across both risk-averse and risk-seeking regimes.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Collecting data from wireless sensors can be challenging when limited communication infrastructure prevents them from offloading their measurements. In such settings, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) can exploit their mobility to harvest data locally, while coordinated UAV teams can provide efficient and redundant coverage of remote areas. Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables such teams to learn decentralized coordination policies. However, training separate policies for different team and sensor populations is costly, and fixed-population policies may be vulnerable to agent failures. This work proposes F-MADDPG, a flexible variant of Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) that supports data harvesting with varying numbers of wireless sensors and UAVs. The framework also accommodates unexpected catastrophic failures that permanently remove agents during a mission. We compare F-MADDPG with a MADDPG baseline that uses multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and evaluate training strategies based on team-size sampling and exposure to agent terminations. The results show that F-MADDPG learns across the evaluated population sizes, completes missions faster than the baseline on average, benefits from inverse-cardinality team-size sampling compared with uniform sampling, remains effective after permanent agent losses, and gains no consistent benefit from exposure to failures during training.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Split-RL_Local_Conflict_Resolution_in_Reinforcement_Learning|Split-RL: Local Conflict Resolution in Reinforcement Learning]]

![[assets/2611.34799_figure.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《Split-RL: Local Conflict Resolution in Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、机器人、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、机器人、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Constrained-RL, GBRL, Offline-RL, SUMO-RL, Split-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning (RL) policies are often trained by mixing contradictory feedback that is localized in space and time. For example, a robot may prioritize speed in open areas versus precision in tight gaps. We introduce Split-RL, a Gradient Boosting Trees (GBT) algorithm that uses guidance labels to locally isolate these conflicting signals. Standard neural networks (NNs) lack the structural mechanism to do this, as their parameters share feedback from all sources. While GBT provides the inductive bias to explicitly partition the state-space per objective, standard tree-fitting procedures aggregate contradictory gradient signals before they reach the leaves. Split-RL leverages this bias to integrate guidance labels into tree construction, routing these updates into disjoint state-space regions and preventing signal interference. Such labels can arise from heuristics, sensor-derived events, or existing rule-based systems, allowing Split-RL to incorporate rule-derived structure while retaining a learned policy. We theoretically show how early gradient aggregation loses objective-specific information and characterize when the Split-RL score isolates conflicting updates. We focus on constrained RL and offline RL with mixed datasets, each balancing two objectives that conflict locally. Across domains with spatial, rare-event, and temporal conflicts, Split-RL consistently outperforms existing methods by meeting the episodic cost budget while maintaining competitive rewards. In offline settings, Split-RL localizes the tradeoff between imitation and policy improvement, outperforming other methods and achieving near-expert performance across varying dataset sizes.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/When_Every_Rollout_Fails_Splicing_Learner-Conditioned_Options_into_Long-Horizon_Agent_RL|When Every Rollout Fails: Splicing Learner-Conditioned Options into Long-Horizon Agent RL]]

![[assets/2611.34777_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34777](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34777)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34777

- **作者**: Haowen Hou, Yicheng Liu, Zhen Huang, Jiaqi Wang
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.LG

#### 研究背景与动机

《When Every Rollout Fails: Splicing Learner-Conditioned Options into Long-Horizon Agent RL》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Group-based reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards often yields all-fail groups on long-horizon agent tasks: every sampled trajectory receives zero reward, leaving group-relative methods without a reward-based policy gradient. A common remedy supplies complete solutions from a stronger model, but full trajectories are expensive and expose the learner to long off-policy histories. We introduce SPLICE, which constructs local training targets from failed current-policy trajectories online. For each all-fail group, a proposal model selects a splice point in a current-policy trajectory and generates several bounded options from that point. We estimate each option's value using repeated current-policy continuations, convert positive estimates into a return-weighted local target, and fit the current policy on the options. We evaluate SPLICE across two policy families on two long-horizon agent tasks, Software Engineering with SWE-bench Verified and Deep Research with BrowseComp-Plus. SPLICE outperforms GRPO and full-trajectory guidance baselines in all four settings, improving over GRPO by 2.8--9.8 percentage points. On initially failed SWE tasks, it raises GRPO's graduation rate by 10.8 percentage points. Further experiments show that SPLICE improves performance while reducing costly proposal-model computation.

</details>

- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG

#### 研究背景与动机

《Maco-RL: Scaling Agent Capabilities with Mixed-Task Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ACuRL, AgentGym, AgentRL, Claw-Eval, CodeWorld, ComputerRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Mixed-task reinforcement learning (MixRL) directly trains a unified policy across domains to continually expand agent capabilities without training separate specialists or requiring an extra distillation stage. Despite its appeal, scaling MixRL poses significant challenges in achieving stable and efficient mixed-task learning, effectively allocating rollout compute, and supporting continual domain expansion. To address these challenges, we propose Maco-RL, an approach that effectively scales MixRL. Specifically, Maco-RL employs a quota-based scheduler for asynchronous rollouts across multiple task domains, thereby stabilizing training by balancing gradient contributions and mitigating off-policy effects. Along an orthogonal axis, Maco-RL further introduces Tri-Buffer Sampling (TBS) to allocate compute among samples with varying levels of mastery, promoting sustained frontier exploration while preserving acquired capabilities. Experiments on five benchmarks show Maco-RL raises Qwen3.5-27B's overall score from 43.6 to 54.2 and then to 60.1 with continual expansion, reaching the frontier held by Qwen3.8-27B, which is further pushed outward by +5.7. Maco-RL also outperforms single-task RL with less compute for policy updates. These results support MixRL as a practical and compute-efficient post-training approach with substantial potential to strengthen general agent capabilities.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Provably_Efficient_Concave_Utility_Reinforcement_Learning_with_Linear_Function_Approximation|Provably Efficient Concave Utility Reinforcement Learning with Linear Function Approximation]]

![[assets/2611.34678_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34678)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34678
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Provably_Efficient_Concave_Utility_Reinforcement_Learning_with_Linear_Function_Approximation|Provably Efficient Concave Utility Reinforcement Learning with Linear Function Approximation]]
- **作者**: Max Qiushi Lin, Ahmed Magd, Sharan Vaswani
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 世界模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.72（加权：强化学习 1.36，世界模型 0.36）

#### 研究背景与动机

《Provably Efficient Concave Utility Reinforcement Learning with Linear Function Approximation》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CURL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Concave utility reinforcement learning (CURL) unifies a diverse range of problems, including standard reinforcement learning (RL), imitation learning, and maximum entropy exploration. Unlike standard RL, CURL generally requires estimating high-dimensional state-action occupancy measures. Consequently, existing methods for CURL either sidestep the exploration problem, are restricted to the tabular setting, or lack computationally efficient policy optimization. To address this, we study CURL in finite-horizon episodic bilinear MDPs, where the transition model admits a low-dimensional representation in known feature spaces. We use the bilinear structure to efficiently estimate occupancy measures via low-dimensional feature expectations, and propose a model-based optimistic concave utility policy optimization (OCUPO) framework. We first instantiate OCUPO in the on-policy setting, where each iteration uses independent trajectories to estimate the concave utility policy gradient, and prove an $\widetilde{O}(ε^{-4})$ sample complexity. To improve sample efficiency, we then instantiate OCUPO in the off-policy setting. We propose a novel backward feature regression subroutine that reuses trajectories collected by past policies to estimate the occupancy measure of the current policy. Under suitable technical assumptions on the policy class (satisfied, e.g., by log-linear policies with sufficiently expressive features), we prove that the resulting algorithm achieves an $\widetilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity, up to a controllable projection error. We further extend our framework to linear MDPs and show that the resulting off-policy algorithm retains the same sample complexity. In these settings, the dependence on $ε$ matches that for standard RL with linear function approximation. Moreover, our algorithms do not rely on the integral oracle required by prior work.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Do We Still Need Reinforcement Learning When We Can Read the World Model?》归入 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：D4RL, PlaNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning has achieved remarkable success in sequential decision-making, but learning effective policies often requires substantial interaction experience. Model-based reinforcement learning alleviates this dependence by leveraging world models, which are typically used to support planning or policy optimization. In this paper, we investigate an alternative paradigm in which autonomous LLM-based coding agents directly synthesize control policies by reasoning over readable world models. By examining environment source code, these agents translate environmental knowledge into executable decision rules and iteratively refine their policies using online feedback or offline data, without task-specific RL training. Experiments across diverse decision-making benchmarks show that synthesized policies match or outperform conventional RL methods while requiring substantially less experience. Further analyses reveal interpretable decision rules and how they evolve through feedback-driven refinement. Comparisons with policies generated without source code access and experiments under challenging conditions further demonstrate the benefits of readable world models. These findings suggest that direct policy synthesis can reduce reliance on trial and error, challenging the necessity of task-specific RL training.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Distractor-Robust Reinforcement Learning via Variational Bisimulation》归入 强化学习、世界模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) promises data efficiency and generalization, but typical reconstruction-based objectives encourage models to waste representational capacity on task-irrelevant distractors. We introduce VIBES (Variational Inference for Bisimulation-based Encoded States), a new objective that replaces pixel reconstruction with an adversarial term, which enforces that latent states suffice to predict both rewards and the next latent state. We show that this objective measures how far the encoder is from reward and dynamics sufficiency, and that whenever exact sufficiency is attainable, its global optima induce bisimulation relations on histories. Combined with a fixed-capacity latent bottleneck, this encourages latent states to retain task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant variation. Our method serves as a drop-in replacement for Dreamer's model-learning component, achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Distracting Control Suite, and extends to visually complex manipulation in Realistic ManiSkill. Unlike prior approaches, it does not rely on image-specific augmentations and applies equally well to high-dimensional vector-state tasks, demonstrated on a 100-link swimmer robot. Finally, latent-space analyses (UMAP embeddings and nearest-neighbor probes) confirm that the learned representations are sensitive to task-relevant structure while invariant to distractors.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/LatentRL-QAS_Transfer_via_Latent_Reinforcement_Learning_for_Quantum_Architecture_Search|LatentRL-QAS: Transfer via Latent Reinforcement Learning for Quantum Architecture Search]]

![[assets/2611.34634_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34634](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34634)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34634
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/LatentRL-QAS_Transfer_via_Latent_Reinforcement_Learning_for_Quantum_Architecture_Search|LatentRL-QAS: Transfer via Latent Reinforcement Learning for Quantum Architecture Search]]
- **作者**: Maria Ilektra Plakitsi, Sebastian Feld, Akash Kundu
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 世界模型, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《LatentRL-QAS: Transfer via Latent Reinforcement Learning for Quantum Architecture Search》归入 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：LatentRL, TensorRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning (RL) emerges as a prominent approach to quantum architecture search (QAS), the automated design of quantum circuits. However, transferring what an agent has learned from one number of qubits to another is still weakly explored: the state, the action space and the neural network of a state-of-the-art RL-QAS agent all grow with the number of qubits, which makes transfer learning across qubit counts prohibitive. We make this transfer possible by introducing LatentRL-QAS, a latent space transfer and refresh framework for RL-based QAS. At its core, a circuit variational autoencoder encodes a circuit as one token per gate and maps circuits on any number of qubits into the same latent space. An RL agent navigates this space instead of adding gates, so its state, actions and network have the same size at every qubit count. The latent space is periodically refreshed with the agent's new discoveries and, for a larger system, grown from the solutions of the smaller one, so what is transferred is the representation of circuits, not network weights or past experience. To show transfer, we consider absolutely maximally entangled (AME) states, a resource for quantum error correction. LatentRL-QAS discovers the five- and six-qubit AME states without being shown them and prepares them in 89-99% of evaluation episodes, whereas the baseline RL-QAS fails to find them with the same budget, even with a network 10 to 17 times larger. LatentRL-QAS remains robust under depolarizing noise with single-qubit gate errors amplified 10x and two-qubit gate errors 5x compared with IBM quantum hardware. By decoupling the RL agent from the number of qubits, LatentRL-QAS lets quantum architecture search reuse circuit representations learned on smaller problems, providing a foundation for the transferable design of circuits across quantum optimization problems.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Kant-Q: A Q-Learning Algorithm for Kantian Equilibrium in Symmetric Markov Games》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：GovSim, MARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

AI agents increasingly interact with one another, and many of these interactions are mixed-motive games, which have posed a persistent challenge for learning algorithms. Most multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithms are designed to reach Nash equilibria, yet in many such games these equilibria are Pareto-inefficient. We instead study Kantian equilibria, an economic solution concept in which each agent plays the action it would want all agents to adopt. In symmetric games, this corresponds to an optimal common policy. We propose Kant-Q, a Q-learning algorithm in which each agent learns action profiles where all agents play the same action. Each update is then a sample from a single-agent MDP, so Kant-Q inherits the convergence guarantees of standard Q-learning. We show that every symmetric Markov game has a pure Kantian equilibrium. We also prove that all agents' Q-tables converge to the same limit, whose greedy policy is such an equilibrium, and that greedy play converges whenever the optimal common action is unique at every state. We further show that in a subclass of symmetric monotone games, the Kantian equilibrium is Pareto-efficient among pure strategy profiles and strictly individually rational. Empirically, on the iterated Prisoner's Dilemma and the GovSim fishery with up to 200 agents, Kant-Q learns the Kantian equilibrium, while the baselines underperform. Since copies of the same AI system naturally face one another in symmetric games, these results suggest universalization as a design principle for cooperative AI-AI interaction.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Feasibility-Aware Deep Reinforcement Learning for O-CU and O-DU Mapping in 6G O-RAN》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This paper studies the mapping of the virtual network function components (VNFCs) of the O-RAN central unit (O-CU) and O-RAN distributed unit (O-DU) onto virtual machines (VMs) in sixth-generation (6G) open radio access networks (O-RAN). The problem is formulated as a feasibility-aware sequential decision process that maximizes the number of placed VNFCs and, secondarily, favors fewer active VMs and lower residual resource wastage. Although conventional optimization techniques can solve this problem, their computational cost becomes prohibitive for larger instances or when optimization must be repeated. To address this limitation, we explore two neural reinforcement learning (RL)-based methods: a deep $Q$-network (DQN) and a double deep $Q$-network (Double DQN). Whereas DQN uses the same target network to both select and evaluate the bootstrap action, Double DQN decouples these two operations to mitigate maximization-induced overestimation. We compare both methods against six baselines: random feasible placement, first-fit, first-fit decreasing, best-fit, a resource-wastage-aware heuristic, and a time-bounded mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) solver. We conduct a controlled experimental evaluation across four problem sizes with $30$ independent instances each, together with capacity-stress, distribution-shift, and reward-ablation experiments. For the $16 \times 8$ VNFC-to-VM scenario, DQN and Double DQN achieve mean placed-VNFC fractions of $0.990$ and $0.996$, respectively. However, first-fit decreasing and the resource-wastage-aware heuristic match this placement quality with lower efficiency cost and lower decision time, whereas the MILP attains the lowest efficiency cost at a much higher runtime. The results clarify the strengths and limitations of neural placement for this static problem, without establishing superiority over conventional methods.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《PROVE: Mitigating Long-Horizon LLM Collusion with Reinforcement Learning from Verified Evidence》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

When LLM agents verify each other's work over a long interaction, they drift into collusion: they come to accept work without the evidence that the verification protocol requires. Collusion is absent at the start and builds up episode by episode, which we trace to accurate feedback accumulating in memory. An agent that follows the rule is told that its verdict was wrong whenever its peer was right, so breaking the rule is the reward-maximizing choice, and agents talk themselves into it in their private reflections. Existing mitigations leave the agents unchanged; we train the behavior out. PROVE (Protocol Reinforcement from Verified Evidence) is a multi-agent reinforcement learning method that rewards verdicts by the evidence behind them rather than by their outcome, and credits every call an agent makes, including the calls that solve its own task, with the outcome that call controls. On Qwen3.6-27B, a LoRA adapter trained on six-episode interactions takes the collusion rate on held-out task sequences at the original ten-episode horizon from 65.0% to 1.0%, with no detectable change in task success. Collusion that converged in every interaction now converges in none, and the gain holds four episodes beyond the longest interaction seen in training. The trained 27B model colludes less than every model evaluated in the original study, closed-weight frontier models included. Code is available at https://github.com/Haochen-Luo/PROVE.

</details>

- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG

#### 研究背景与动机

《Post-Training Policy Replication for Strategy-Following Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning across Team Sizes》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DRL, MADRL, MARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Although multi-agent deep reinforcement learning enables coordinated behavior, aligning it with managerial intent remains difficult due to complex agent interactions. Although the strategy-following distributed attentional actor architecture (sfDA7-X) improves controllability via regional instructions, instruction combinations scale exponentially with team and instruction sizes, leading to prohibitive training costs and poor transferability. To reduce this computational cost, we introduce a team-size-independent observation architecture and reconstruct collective behavior by replicating a single trained policy across agents in teams of different sizes, thereby eliminating the need to train from scratch. Additionally, we evaluate warm-start retraining by initializing policy networks with a pre-trained policy to significantly lower computational overhead. In an object collection game, a policy trained in a four-agent team is deployed to teams of three to eight agents. Experiments demonstrate that training with the proposed aggregated observation matrix achieves performance comparable to that of the baseline method, sfDA7-X. Furthermore, policy replication preserves performance without substantial degradation in three- and five-agent teams, and warm-start retraining outperforms training from scratch while reducing required training episodes by up to 90%. Deployments to six- and eight-agent teams further confirm effective spatial region allocation following given instructions. These results demonstrate that the proposed method enables zero-shot collective behavior reconstruction via policy replication.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Differentially Private Policy Optimization in Linear MDPs with Adversarial Rewards》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning (RL) in personalized services often relies on sensitive interaction data. Motivated by the need to protect these data, we study differentially private (DP) policy optimization in episodic linear Markov decision processes (MDPs) with unknown transitions and adaptive adversarial linear rewards under full-information feedback. This setting poses two key challenges: past trajectories are repeatedly reused for policy evaluation, and reward choices may depend on previously released private statistics. The proposed algorithm combines batched proximal policy optimization (PPO) updates with optimistic policy evaluation, using private regression statistics and reward estimates to protect both trajectories and reward functions under joint differential privacy (JDP). With all other parameters fixed, our high-probability regret bound retains the same leading $\widetilde{O}\left(K^{3/4}\right)$ dependence on the number of episodes $K$ as the corresponding non-private bound for batched optimistic PPO. The additional privacy terms scale as $\widetilde{O} \left(K^{5/8}\right)$ and $\widetilde{O} \left(K^{1/2}\right)$. Thus, privacy incurs only lower-order additive costs in $K$, even under adaptive adversarial rewards.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《A Semantics-First Approach to Multimodal Video Captioning with Frozen Encoders and Stabilized Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computation and Language 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Computation and Language 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We propose the Audio-Visual Query Transformer (AVQFormer), a highly efficient architecture built upon a 2.7B parameter LLM featuring a fixed-length $K=16$ semantic alignment module, stabilized via Adaptive Self-Critical Sequence Training (ASCST) with Entropy Regularization. Multimodal Large Language Models (LLMs) often rely on global audio representations to compensate for inefficient, frame-based visual encoders. While our Phase 1 prompted evaluations demonstrate that the Multimodal Base Model achieves strong deep semantic alignment (scoring 0.889 on BERTScore F1), optimizing this architecture for legacy metrics reveals a performance gap. We explore this gap as a hypothesis we term temporal alignment friction: a conflict where global audio representations disrupt the chronological sequence of visual frames, leading to penalties under visually-focused evaluation metrics. Consequently, by stripping away the audio, our Video-Only ablation conforms more closely to this rigid syntax, achieving 63.0 CIDEr. While trailing larger models such as Qwen2.5-VL (67.4 CIDEr), this establishes a strong baseline for sub-3B parameter LLM-based models. This indicates that CIDEr, as applied to datasets such as MSR-VTT, may exhibit a bias toward visually grounded syntax, limiting its ability to fully capture multimodal semantic richness.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ActionFaultBench, DreamSim, DrivingWorld, MiraBench, Vid2World, World-in-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models are evaluated by predicting future observations from actions. These evaluations assume that actions are prepared the same way as during training, but an unchecked mismatch can make a model appear less accurate than it is. We audit this assumption in Navigation World Models (NWM) and find three interacting differences between its training and rollout code. Correcting the inputs, without retraining, reduces mean visual prediction error by 42%, measured with DreamSim on the RECON navigation dataset. The correction also changes the ranking of NWM checkpoints in an exploratory analysis and removes or reduces two alternative models' measured advantages. Correcting only the normalization offset instead increases error, showing why interacting mismatches must be checked together. To test whether sensitivity to input preparation extends beyond NWM, we deliberately introduce encoding errors into two alternative models; both become less accurate. We introduce ActRosetta, a tool that checks whether action encoding matches training, identifies supported causes of disagreement, and repairs inputs when sufficient information is available. Repair helps little where predictions barely respond to actions, the tool's diagnosis has limited coverage, and improved control remains unproven. For any action-conditioned world model, the lesson is the same: before reporting rollout error, verify that evaluation encodes actions exactly as training did.

</details>

- **作者**: Devesh Nath, Glen Chou
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 世界模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《$μ$MPC: Microsecond-Scale Nonlinear and World Model Control from Edge to GPU via Koopman-Linearized Precompilation》归入 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present $μ$MPC, a fast model predictive control (MPC) framework that reduces online computation using learned Koopman-linearized dynamics. By precomputing finite-horizon control-to-output maps, $μ$MPC eliminates lifted-state trajectories, yielding an action-only Koopman-Compiled QP (KC-QP) with $Hn_u$ decision variables, independent of lifted dimension. A fixed, precomputed primal operator enables efficient primal-dual iterations using only matrix-vector products and projections, accommodating changing goals and constraints without refactorization. This efficiency also enables control-aware dynamics learning through differentiable KC-QPs in the training loop and batched DAgger-style data aggregation. $μ$MPC achieves solve times as low as $23μ$s on a desktop CPU and 24 ms onboard a 168 MHz Crazyflie microcontroller. It scales to 4096D quadruped control in under 5 ms, visual planning with 2048D world models in 0.4 ms, and collision-aware planning for 200 quadrotors at over 30 Hz on the GPU. Across these benchmarks, $μ$MPC achieves up to $108\times$ faster constrained MPC and $1907\times$ faster visuomotor planning than competing methods.

</details>

- **作者**: Syed Nazmus Sakib, Abdul Monaf Chowdhury, Peizhen Li, Nafiul Haque, Chandak Chakma, Md Mehedi Hasan, Zehong Cao, Shifat E. Arman
