#### 研究背景与动机

《Long-Horizon Planning with Latent World Models via Graph Anchors》归入 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Meta-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Latent world models plan in a compact latent space by predicting the outcomes of actions. Their planners judge a plan by the latent distance between its predicted end state and the goal image. We show that this distance fails in two distinct ways. First, it loses directional signal a few steps from the goal, causing distant goals to fail. Second, it weighs the robot arm over the manipulated object, causing manipulation to fail at contact. We measure both failures directly on recorded data by asking whether a lower latent distance corresponds to true progress toward the goal. Across five environments, six visual representations (including raw pixels and V-JEPA 2), and nine Meta-World tasks, this ranking test cleanly separates the two failures, and the graph distance to a goal predicts where closed-loop planning breaks down. Both failures share a single remedy: replacing the distant goal with a nearby target that lies on a recorded trajectory. We realize this principle with Graph Anchors, a training-free planner for frozen latent world models. It builds a sparse graph once over the model's own training frames and aims each planning step at a node a few moves ahead along the shortest path to the goal, keeping paths on recorded transitions wherever contact matters. On goals up to 400 steps away, Graph Anchors raise LeWM's success from 13% to 85% on PointMaze-Medium, from 8% to 87% on PointMaze-Large, and from 5% to 71% on Push-T, where stronger search toward the goal fails and where prior training-free planners break down under missing step horizons, subsampling shortcuts, or fragmented contact.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Co-World Model for Human-Video-to-Robot Transfer via Co-Constructed Shared Latent Dynamics》归入 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：结果验证了方案可行性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Co-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Human videos are abundant and cheap to obtain, whereas robot trajectories remain scarce and costly, making zero-shot skill transfer from a single human demonstration an appealing route to scalable robot learning. Existing methods, however, either retarget human-specific geometry or match only static visual goals, and thus stumble over the cross-embodiment dynamics mismatch and the ungrounded action execution. We present the Co-World Model (CoWM), a predictive framework jointly optimized from paired yet unsynchronized human and robot streams. To bridge the cross-embodiment dynamics gap, CoWM disentangles each observation into a shared object-relation state and embodiment-specific context. It aligns latent actions across embodiments using effect-swap and action-sensitivity objectives,such that actions are matched by their predicted effects on the manipulated objects rather than by embodimentdependent kinematics. For grounded execution, a phase-aware receding-horizon controller estimates demonstration progress, projects the inferred human action prior onto the robot action-support density, and translates the resulting latent dynamics into executable robot commands. Extensive experiments in simulation and on two physical robot platforms demonstrate the effectiveness of CoWM, its cross-platform generalizability, and the contribution of each component. See the demo video in the supporting information for details.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Normalizing Flows are Capable World Models》归入 世界模型、强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A world model lets an agent anticipate the consequences of its actions and use imagined futures to make decisions. We introduce Normalizing-Flow World Model (NFWM), which pairs an invertible observation encoder with an action-conditioned probabilistic predictor. Because the encoder is invertible, the predicted distribution over latent states defines a distribution over future observations. Maximizing the exact conditional likelihood of those observations therefore trains the representation and dynamics jointly. An agent can plan directly in the learned latent space and decode sampled trajectories to inspect possible futures. On Teleport, these samples capture the two possible outcomes of the same action. In Flappy Bird, a world model trained by likelihood alone supports reward-based planning that reaches 100 pipes in all ten evaluated games. For goal-conditioned control, an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective shapes the coordinates used to compare predicted states with a goal. On LIBERO, NFWM uses this distance to select successful action sequences more often than LeWM and SMWM. In Atari Boxing, training on imagined trajectories improves a policy beyond its behavior-cloned initialization. Together, these results establish normalizing flows as capable world models.

</details>

![[assets/2611.33745_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33745](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33745)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33745
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/WhatIf_Counterfactual_World_Models_over_Action-Grounded_Causal_Graphs_for_Robotic_Manipulation|WhatIf: Counterfactual World Models over Action-Grounded Causal Graphs for Robotic Manipulation]]
- **作者**: Xu Yang, Kailai Sun, Qianchuan Zhao
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI
- **归属领域**: 世界模型
- **相关领域**: 世界模型, 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.5（加权：具身智能 0.9，世界模型 1，机器人 0.6）

#### 研究背景与动机

《WhatIf: Counterfactual World Models over Action-Grounded Causal Graphs for Robotic Manipulation》归入 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models predict what happens under an action, but choosing between actions requires comparing their outcomes in the same physical situation. Factual transitions alone determine action-wise marginal futures and average effects, but not individual effects or the probability that switching actions causes harm. We introduce WhatIf, a counterfactual world model for manipulation that couples candidate futures through shared latent physics and transition noise inferred from factual history. Each candidate action induces a sparse object-factor causal graph describing the physical pathways it activates. Rolling out these graphs under the shared situation anchors predictions to observed outcomes and confines action contrasts to the objects each action reaches. Shared type-conditioned functions support varying numbers of objects, while a round-trip loss enforces action-invariant abduction without counterfactual labels. We show that replacing an individual effect with its conditional mean incurs mean-squared error equal to its conditional variance, motivating comparisons between coupled futures. Building on these comparisons, Counterfactual Advantage Planning (CAP) ranks plans by their advantage over an incumbent within shared situations and constrains the probability of harm. Paired advantages reduce estimator variance by a factor of $1-ρ$, where $ρ$ is the correlation between branch outcomes, and cancel model errors shared by both branches. Each executed transition provides hindsight advantages to update the physics posterior, the next plan, and an amortized policy without a learned critic. We further establish when observational transitions identify interventional dynamics. Paired simulator interventions evaluate counterfactual accuracy, graph recovery, compositional generalization, and decision quality.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/WorldUAV_Scaling_UAV_Data_Synthesis_via_World_Models|WorldUAV: Scaling UAV Data Synthesis via World Models]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《WorldUAV: Scaling UAV Data Synthesis via World Models》归入 世界模型、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

UAV detection and tracking remain constrained by the scarcity of high-quality training data. Existing game-engine-based synthetic UAV data suffer from a substantial synthetic-to-real gap, while the high cost of manually collected real-world UAV data limits dataset scale. Therefore, we introduce WorldUAV, a high-quality UAV dataset constructed through an efficient framework powered by world models. Specifically, WorldUAV directly integrates UAVs into readily available real-world videos, naturally preserving realistic backgrounds, illumination, and scene dynamics. Leveraging the spatiotemporal priors of world models, it generates UAV videos with plausible motion, pose variations, and cross-frame consistency, effectively narrowing the gap between synthetic and real data. We further develop an agentic pipeline orchestrated by a multimodal large language model that acquires sources, plans UAV trajectories, invokes synthesis, and verifies generated videos, enabling fully automated dataset construction. WorldUAV comprises 6,878 UAV videos and 205,778 UAV images. Experiments show that WorldUAV significantly improves the performance of UAV detectors. Moreover, it substantially reduces dataset construction costs, requiring only 15 seconds and USD 0.0005 API cost per high-quality UAV image. Our work offers both a high-quality dataset and a scalable data generation paradigm for UAV detection and tracking.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/SplatWAM_Splatting_World_Action_Models_for_Real-Time_3D_Imagination|SplatWAM: Splatting World Action Models for Real-Time 3D Imagination]]

![[assets/2611.33467_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33467](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33467)

- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/SplatWAM_Splatting_World_Action_Models_for_Real-Time_3D_Imagination|SplatWAM: Splatting World Action Models for Real-Time 3D Imagination]]
- **作者**: Frank Tsu-Fang Lu, Karthik Sundar Senthilkumar, Christopher Thirgood, Oscar Mendez, Simon Hadfield
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 世界模型
- **相关领域**: 世界模型, 机器人, 具身智能, 强化学习

- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL, WorldModel

#### 研究背景与动机

《SplatWAM: Splatting World Action Models for Real-Time 3D Imagination》归入 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OGBench, OpenVLA, PointWorld, WorldVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models typically predict future observations in pixel or latent space, limiting their ability to directly represent true 3D scene evolution. We present SplatWAM (Splatting World Action Model), a generative world model that learns action-conditioned dynamics directly over compact 3D Gaussian primitives. A tokenizer SplatWAM-T first encodes a variable number of unposed views into a fixed budget of Gaussian tokens. A dynamics model SplatWAM-D then predicts future Gaussian states in a single forward pass per action, using log-Euclidean covariance coordinates and differentiable rendering supervision to facilitate learning in an explicit 3D space. Each predicted state can be rasterized from arbitrary viewpoints, enforcing a shared 3D representation across views by construction. On OGBench Scene, SplatWAM renders novel views 12x faster than video world models (~80 FPS on an RTX 5090) with up to 2.0 dB higher PSNR. We further introduce SplatWAM-P, a lightweight flow-matching policy head jointly trained with the dynamics model. Across 8 task suites, model-free Gaussian-token policies outperform pixel-based counterparts by up to 24.8 percentage points. SplatWAM-P improves over Latent Particle World Model (LPWM) by 12 percentage points and matches HIQL without a learned value function. On LIBERO, our ~1B model outperforms WorldVLA (~7B) and the robot-pretrained OpenVLA (~7B), while matching OpenVLA-OFT when both are trained without large-scale robot pretraining. Code and checkpoints will be released.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《MERLoc: Robust Robot Localization in Dynamic Environments with Pose-Based Retrieval over 3D Gaussian Splatting References》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robots operating in previously mapped spaces must localize accurately despite moving people and rearranged objects. Most localization pipelines match the query against a sparse set of captured reference images retrieved by appearance, which is brittle under scene change and repeating structures. We present MERLoc, which turns a single metric 3D Gaussian Splatting scan into a dense Functional Reference Memory. Reference views are sampled over the navigable space for the robot's morphology and sensors at a target density and rendered offline. Pre-encoding them for the chosen off-the-shelf localizer removes the reference encoder from inference. A pose-based retrieval strategy then selects references by spatial proximity and viewing direction rather than appearance, sidestepping visual ambiguities whenever a prior exists. Across one classical and four learned localizers in simulated and real dynamic scenes, pose-based retrieval matches or outperforms appearance retrieval in most cases, even when the latter is gated by the same prior. On real data, it lowers position RMSE by 15-60 % in nine of ten backend/split combinations, and the rendered memory outperforms real-image caches posed by Structure-from-Motion. The system runs onboard a Jetson Orin NX at 5 Hz, enabling a navigation policy to successfully traverse a hallway with people roaming and rearranging objects.

</details>

- **作者**: Shuaijun Liu, Qifu Wen, Xingwei Chen, Xinwen Hu, Feiyang You, Sijia Chen, Ningxin Su

#### 研究背景与动机

《One Ocean, Many Tasks: A Data-Grounded Simulation Suite and Benchmark for Marine Robotics》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Simulation is essential for marine robotics, yet existing platforms often trade off between visually realistic worlds, high-throughput experimentation, and ocean-condition realism grounded in real data. We present OneOcean, a data-grounded simulation suite and benchmark built around a unified spatiotemporal ocean-environment product that harmonizes bathymetry with data-driven currents and tides, with optional pollution fields for cleanup-related evaluation. On top of this environment representation, OneOcean defines a task ladder spanning navigation and station-keeping under currents, waypoint and route following, depth-profile tracking, area scanning, pipeline inspection, and multi-agent coordination, including formation transit, fish protection and patrol (herding), and surface pollution localization, containment, and cleanup. The suite supports multi-agent settings with 2-10 vehicles. Our simulator supports three to six Degrees of Freedom (DoF) and produces standardized metrics and reproducible run manifests, enabling consistent aggregation across tasks, difficulties, and scaling settings. Experiments across dataset variants and scenes systematically stress planning and control under data-grounded disturbances and reveal trade-offs among heuristic baselines, Behavior Cloning (BC), and a Large Language Model (LLM)-based planner.

</details>

![[assets/2611.34778_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34778](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34778)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34778

- **作者**: Yuxiang Lai, Jike Zhong, Yu-Jhe Li, Lan Wang, Chengzhi Liu, Youbao Tang, Ming Li, Xi Xiao, Xiaofeng Yang, Liang Zhao, Zheda Mai, Ruixi Fan
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Toward Agentic Robotics: A Large-Scale Study of Harness Design for Robot-Use Agents》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MME-VLA, VLABench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recent vision-language models (VLMs) can act as robot-use agents that understand diverse scenes and control robots through policy code and tools. However, they predict 3D gripper poses poorly even with extended reasoning, struggling with precise manipulation. Vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action (WAM) models are the opposite: they execute fast, smooth control on trained tasks, yet fail under domain shifts or when tasks require long-horizon memory and reasoning. Agentic robots therefore combine VLM reasoning and perception with precise VLA/WAM control through a policy harness. Yet harness design spans thousands of configurations, shifting success by tens of points with the same models. Moreover, testing even one design takes thousands of rollouts at high API and GPU cost, so prior works often commit to a single design chosen from few alternatives. To guide harness design, we evaluate over 470,000 rollouts across eight simulation benchmarks, six VLMs, and 15 VLA/WAM checkpoints. Seven controlled studies show effective harnesses must adapt to both VLM and VLA/WAM capabilities. Our findings specify what structure the VLM's policy code needs, where VLA manipulation needs help from tools and policy code, and when to involve the VLM at test time. The reference recipe X-Harness shows that these findings compose without weight updates, lifting base policies up to $+42.8$ points across memory, reasoning, and domain shifts.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/SOLVE_Sparse,_Ordered_Keyposes_Make_LLM_Agents_Versatile_and_Efficient_Robot_Manipulator|SOLVE: Sparse, Ordered Keyposes Make LLM Agents Versatile and Efficient Robot Manipulator]]

![[assets/2611.34750_figure.png|800]]

- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《SOLVE: Sparse, Ordered Keyposes Make LLM Agents Versatile and Efficient Robot Manipulator》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：RoboDojo-Sim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

LLM agents have made remarkable progress in robot manipulation, yet they suffer from expensive inference, dense step-by-step control, and tool bottlenecks. We introduce SOLVE, a training-free, versatile and efficient agent policy: a multimodal LLM agent writes a sparse, ordered sequence of 6-DoF end-effector keyposes per call, observes the result, and writes the next, while a motion planner interpolates between them. The agent decides the number and spacing of keyposes for both fast long-range motion and fine-grained manipulation. This granularity matches the sparsity of teleoperated manipulation: most demonstrations in DROID can be compressed into a few keyposes instead of dense actions. SOLVE grounds keyposes with sparse depth queries answered by noisy sources, such as multi-view triangulation, stereo, or monocular depth, and copes with that noise itself. It also improves from execution feedback, reflecting on its own rollouts and distilling a few rules per task. SOLVE reaches state-of-the-art performance of 74.5% on RoboDojo and 84.5% on RoboLab, with only $1.7 per episode on average, 12x less than previous dense-control agent policies. On a real robot, the same agent achieves an 88% success rate (75 of 85 episodes) over 16 challenging zero-shot tasks. Our project page is at https://serenos.github.io/solve-website.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/RoboEvo$^2$_Recursive_Robot_Self-Improvement_via_Harness-Policy_Co-Evolution|RoboEvo$^2$: Recursive Robot Self-Improvement via Harness-Policy Co-Evolution]]

![[assets/2611.34675_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.34675](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34675)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34675
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/RoboEvo$^2$_Recursive_Robot_Self-Improvement_via_Harness-Policy_Co-Evolution|RoboEvo$^2$: Recursive Robot Self-Improvement via Harness-Policy Co-Evolution]]
- **作者**: Chenzhi Liu, Jiehong Lin, Wei Huang, Xuzhao Huang, Haoyuan Li, Yang Zhang, Junchao Huang, Chenyeyang Cai, Bo Wang, Kazuhiro Kosuge, Zhongrui Wang, Xiaojuan Qi
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.9（加权：具身智能 0.6，大模型 0.2，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Agent, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《RoboEvo$^2$: Recursive Robot Self-Improvement via Harness-Policy Co-Evolution》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CRL-VLA, PackNet, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robots should adapt to unfamiliar environments and acquire new skills through interaction, then execute those skills efficiently with practice. Vision-language-action policies provide efficient control, but often need additional human demonstrations to handle unfamiliar settings; reasoning agents can discover solutions using broad prior knowledge and robot-control APIs, but repeated deliberation and repair are costly. Inspired by human learning through reasoning and practice, we propose RoboEvo$^2$, a framework for recursive harness-policy co-evolution. In Policy-to-Harness (P2H) evolution, policy failures guide a coding agent to search complementary Code, Code+ICL, and Code+Policy routes, consolidating verified solutions into reusable skills, routing strategies, and experience memory. In Harness-to-Policy (H2P) evolution, verified trajectories provide supervised training, while Counterfactual Preference Distillation (CPD) teaches the policy to favor successful corrections over failures from the same starting state. The improved policy provides stronger motor skills for subsequent search, while its remaining failures redirect harness evolution, closing the reciprocal loop. Through recursive co-evolution without additional human demonstrations, RoboEvo$^2$ improves standalone success from 11.0% to 84.1% and hybrid success from 30.4% to 90.5% on LIBERO-PRO, with similar gains and reduced agentic costs on RoboTwin C2R. Further experiments demonstrate autonomous task acquisition, cross-embodiment harness transfer, and 95.0% hybrid success on the Unitree G1-D. Our models and code will be publicly released.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/DemoFoundry_Toward_Scalable_Photorealistic_Robot_Data_with_Coding_Agents_and_Generative_Rendering|DemoFoundry: Toward Scalable Photorealistic Robot Data with Coding Agents and Generative Rendering]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《DemoFoundry: Toward Scalable Photorealistic Robot Data with Coding Agents and Generative Rendering》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ComSim, DexSim, PointWorld, Real2Sim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Scaling robot learning requires demonstrations that combine realistic visual observations with reliable action supervision. Simulation-based synthesis faces a substantial visual gap relative to real environments, while video-only generation struggles to maintain physically consistent interactions and provide aligned action labels. We present DemoFoundry, an automated framework that couples coding agents with generative rendering to synthesize photorealistic and physically grounded robot demonstrations. Coding agents both construct aligned real--proxy video pairs from existing robot episodes to train a video renderer and write executable interaction programs to generate new demonstrations. Given a scene reference and task instruction, an agent reconstructs task-relevant geometry and writes a control program without requiring target-task human demonstrations. Program execution in a physics simulator produces proxy videos with aligned robot states and actions. The video renderer translates these proxy videos into scene-consistent, photorealistic observations, reducing the need for detailed appearance modeling in each scene. Automated task-execution checks select successful trajectories for rendering and policy learning. Experiments show improved visual quality and action--video consistency. Policies trained on the generated demonstrations outperform raw-simulation training and approach the performance of training on an equal number of real demonstrations.

</details>

- **作者**: Luca Beber, Cem Bilaloglu, Edoardo Lamon, Matteo Saveriano, Daniele Fontanelli, Luigi Palopoli, Sylvain Calinon

#### 研究背景与动机

《Autonomous Ergodic Robotic Palpation for Elastic Mapping of Curved Soft Tissues》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Autonomous robotic palpation offers repeatable, quantitative estimates of tissue properties for stiff inclusion localization. However, existing methods often rely on prior mesh models or planar surface assumptions that do not generalize to complex human anatomy. We propose an ergodic palpation framework that operates directly on curved soft surfaces using point cloud data. Central to our approach is the Laplace--Beltrami operator, which grounds both online estimation and exploration in the surface intrinsic geometry. We estimate local viscoelastic parameters using an Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) and fuse them into a surface-aware Gaussian Process (GP). This probabilistic model drives a diffusion-based ergodic motion planner, generating a continuous exploration trajectory that feeds the new informative samples back to the GP in a closed loop. Throughout this process, a hybrid force-impedance controller regulates interaction forces to maintain contact. We evaluate the approach against existing methods in simulation and validate it in physical experiments on tissue-mimicking silicone phantoms with varying geometries and viscoelastic properties, demonstrating geometry-consistent mapping and stiff inclusion localization under contact-rich conditions.

</details>

- **作者**: Yiming Wang, Yenan Wu, Junda Wu, Yanke Sun, Huizhe Li, Ke Yu, Youmin Gong, Guangfu Ma, Jie Mei
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《Reference-Model-Guided Distributed MPC for UAV Swarm Formation Flying Under Switching Directed Graphs》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Distributed formation control of unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms in complex environments faces several challenges, including switching inter-agent connectivity, limited communication, external disturbances, and collision avoidance. To address these challenges, we propose a reference-model-guided distributed model predictive control (MPC) framework for UAV swarm formation. Each UAV uses relative-position information from its neighbors as input to a local reference model, whose output serves as the formation guidance for the onboard MPC controller. Meanwhile, a Kalman filter is utilized to predict neighboring UAV motions, enabling the construction of inter-UAV collision-avoidance constraints solely from instantaneous relative-state information. By decoupling MPC optimization from distributed coordination, this control architecture preserves the look-ahead planning and constraint-handling capabilities of MPC while enhancing the swarm's adaptability to switching directed graphs. Moreover, eliminating the exchange of neighbors' predicted trajectories reduces communication bandwidth demands, offering strong scalability for large-scale UAV swarms. To improve robustness and safety, disturbance-observer-based feedforward compensation is employed, while the bounded residual disturbance is explicitly considered in the control barrier function (CBF)-based obstacle-avoidance constraints. Finally, the proposed framework is validated through both simulations and real-world flight experiments. Specifically, the flight verification is conducted on a fully distributed multi-UAV platform without external global localization.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Rehearse in 3D, Render in 2D: Physically Consistent and Editable Robot World Models》归入 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Ctrl-World, PointWorld, RehearsalWorld, UniSim, WorldGym。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Embodied world models aim to predict future interactions that are both visually realistic and physically consistent. Yet explicit 3D motion prediction alone does not guarantee that object structure and contact relationships are preserved. Beyond motion prediction, 3D representations provide an explicit state space for imposing structural and physical constraints. Building on this insight, we introduce RehearsalWorld, an editable robot world model driven by 3D Interaction Rehearsal (3DIR). Given an initial RGB-D observation and robot actions, Interaction-Consistent Rehearsal (ICR) uses a pretrained 3D point-flow model as a motion prior. It refines the predicted trajectories through response-aware object grouping, persistent grasp memory, and contact structure refiner, preserving scene stability, object structure, and grasp attachments. Rehearsal-Aligned Video Generation (RAVG) then uses geometric projections of the rehearsal as temporal conditioning. We fine-tune a video generator on projection-video pairs to synthesize realistic interaction videos. Thanks to the decoupling of physical response from visual synthesis, RehearsalWorld also supports independent editing of material properties and camera viewpoints. Compared with PointWorld on real DROID grasping sequences, RehearsalWorld reduces gripper-relative offset by approximately 79% to 84% across deformable, articulated, and rigid objects and lowers reference motion error. For video prediction, it achieves a PSNR of 23.92 dB and an FVD of 149.

</details>

- **作者**: Spencer Sweeney, Hongbo Li, Michael Ziegltrum, Dimitra Koumoutsou, Andromachi Maria Delfaki, Taskin Padir, Dimitrios Kanoulas

#### 研究背景与动机

《Autonomous Vineyard Exploration using LiDAR Sensing: A Field Evaluation on Legged-Wheeled Quadruped Robot》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Autonomous navigation in vineyards presents significant challenges due to uneven terrain, sparse vegetation, and the repetitive geometric nature of vineyard rows. This paper evaluates a LiDAR-based autonomous exploration system deployed on a Unitree Go2 Wheeled quadruped robot. Our approach utilizes an iterative RANSAC-based line detection pipeline to identify vineyard rows and a finite-state navigation strategy to govern row selection and traversal. Field evaluations conducted at Denbies Vineyard (UK) demonstrate the system's ability to perform sustained autonomous row following, completing a 370m up-and-down traversal without intervention. We analyze the failure modes encountered, specifically focusing on line detection instability in sparse vegetation and the trade-offs of spatial filtering. The results suggest that, while RANSAC provides a robust geometric primitive, additional temporal filtering and adaptive perception are required for reliable lane-to-lane transitions in commercial vineyard environments. A video of the experimental results is available at: youtu.be/1om1HFGF4ps

</details>

- **作者**: Licheng Yang, Yonghao He, Lingfeng Qian, Xin Shi, Wei Sui, Hu Su
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Ground Your Steps: Optimizing Sampling Schedules in Flow Matching Robot Policies》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Flow matching robot policies generate action chunks by integrating a learned velocity field at inference. However, under a fixed budget of neural function evaluations (NFEs), the integration result depends on the sampling schedule. This motivates optimizing the schedule to bring the generated action distribution closer to the demonstrations and improve task success. We propose Ground Your Steps (GYS), a training-free method that optimizes the sampling schedule by matching the generated and demonstrated chunk distributions at each observation. GYS applies truncated fractional differencing along each arm joint's action sequence and compares two types of pairwise differences between action chunks at each observation: differences of a generated chunk against a demonstration, and differences of two independent generated chunks against each other. After fractional differencing, these two pairwise-difference distributions coincide exactly when the distributions of generated and demonstrated action chunks match. GYS minimizes the energy distance between these distributions to obtain an improved schedule for each task and NFE budget. Compared with the uniform grid, GYS brings the generated chunk distribution closer to the demonstrated distribution and raises the success rate through the sampling schedule alone. On RoboTwin 2.0 benchmarks, GYS improves success rates at nearly every budget, with gains of 2.8 percentage points for both $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.7. The gains also transfer to three real-world manipulation tasks, reaching up to 3.3 percentage points. Code is available at https://github.com/D-Robotics-AI-Lab/GroundYourSteps.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ABot-PhysWorld, PAI-Bench, PhyGenBench, RBench, ReWorld, ReWorldBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models are increasingly used in robotics to improve the performance of embodied models. Compared with general-purpose video generation, robotics places more stringent requirements on world models. Generated videos must satisfy fine-grained physical constraints on robot motion and object interactions. However, existing world model benchmarks can overlook robotics specific physical details and struggle to assess them accurately. To address this problem, we introduce WorldPhy, a world model benchmark for accurate, fine-grained evaluation of physical plausibility in robotics. Our benchmark contains 300 tasks, each consisting of an initial-frame image and a task instruction, covering diverse robotics scenes, task categories, viewpoints, and robot configurations. We further collect over 12K high-quality human annotations, and use them to train WorldPhyReward to accurately provide scores and reasons across six fine-grained physical dimensions and an instruction-following dimension. Comparisons with general VLMs and existing reward models show that WorldPhyReward better aligns with human ratings. We system- atically evaluate 13 frontier open and closed-source world models. The results reveal that current world models struggle to consistently maintain fine-grained physical plausibility across different robotics scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/chuziyuan139/ WorldPhy.

</details>

![[assets/2611.34043_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Kwunhang Wong, Yang Bai, Qingyang Bao, Liudi Yang, Chenzhi Liu, Jiehong Lin, Wei Huang, Lin Li, Ziyuan Liu, Yujie Yang, Gitta Kutyniok, Zhongrui Wang...

#### 研究背景与动机

《WISER-RL: A Wake-Sleep Learning Mechanism for Robot Reinforcement Learning from One Demonstration》归入 机器人、强化学习、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、强化学习、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DSRL, IBRL, MemoryVLA, Real-World, WISER-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A robot learning from one human demonstration may rarely succeed on its own, leaving reinforcement learning (RL) with little reward feedback. We introduce WISER-RL ({W}ake--sleep policy improvement loop, {I}mitation, and {S}elective {E}xperience {R}eplay), a composite reinforcement learning framework that turns assisted practice into autonomous control. During wake, GPTDAgger monitors disagreement among actions sampled by a diffusion policy. When disagreement crosses a threshold, a frozen GPT controller uses retrieved experience to continue the episode. During sleep, Conflict-Aware Experience Reweighting (CAER) compares visually matched successful trajectories and downweights conflicting action windows with more steps remaining to success. The policy imitates the reweighted successes and learns from both successes and failures through offline advantage-guided RL before collecting the next round. After final online PPO refinement, the autonomous policy succeeded in 100% evaluations on each of six LIBERO tasks and 100% trials on each of two real-robot tasks.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/Beyond_the_Happy_Path_Generalizing_Robot_World_Models_from_Simulated_Failures|Beyond the Happy Path: Generalizing Robot World Models from Simulated Failures]]

![[assets/2611.33925_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33925)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33925
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/Beyond_the_Happy_Path_Generalizing_Robot_World_Models_from_Simulated_Failures|Beyond the Happy Path: Generalizing Robot World Models from Simulated Failures]]
- **作者**: Zhiyue Zhao, Mingyu Liu, Bolin Zhang, Liyang Li, Guangyu Huang, Canyu Zhao, Jinchuan Shi, Haokai Zhang, Hao Chen, Chunhua Shen
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 世界模型, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 2.2（加权：具身智能 0.3，世界模型 0.8，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《Beyond the Happy Path: Generalizing Robot World Models from Simulated Failures》归入 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ABot-PhysWorld, Ctrl-World, EWMBench, IRASim, PlaNet, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot world models are increasingly used to evaluate and plan policies, yet robot training data are dominated by successful demonstrations. Off the happy path, when a grasp is misaligned or mistimed and the object is pushed, tilted, or slips, their predictions break down. Across five pretrained action-conditioned models, object-motion error is at most 10.4% lower than that of a static baseline that copies the first frame, and two models are worse than this baseline. We present WorldReforge, which teaches world models what happens when manipulation goes wrong. We construct the Reforge dataset of 62k simulated trajectories by perturbing reference grasps in position, orientation, and closure timing, recording each resulting push, slip, or failed pickup with ground-truth object pose, contact, and surface points. WorldReforge turns this ground truth into two training signals without additional inference cost. Interaction Alignment focuses reconstruction on the small object and contact regions that a global loss underweights, and Surface Correspondence trains backbone features to follow the same physical surface points across frames. On Cosmos3 Nano, WorldReforge reduces object trajectory error by 28.2% overall and by 35.0% on unseen objects, and it lowers the error in predicting how two actions' outcomes diverge by 43.3%. Although trained only in simulation, it also improves reconstruction on DROID and on real failure videos. Controlled data studies show that failed contact and successful holding teach complementary physics: failed contact teaches how objects respond, and holding teaches how a grasped state persists. Our code, data, and models are available at aim-uofa.github.io/WorldReforge/.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《X-World: Transferring Video Priors to Cross-Embodiment Robot Control》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AgiBotWorld, OpenVLA, X-VLA, X-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Language-instructed manipulation requires robust closedloop policies, yet action-labeled robot trajectories are costly and limited in coverage. Human and robot videos provide complementary spatiotemporal priors for crossembodiment control. We propose X-World, which adapts pretrained video diffusion to variable views. A Q-Former compresses hidden states into tokens for a shared flowmatching expert that predicts action chunks. Datasetspecific projections map native states and actions. We adapt the backbone on large-scale robot and human videos, freeze it for action-only pretraining of the Q-Former and action expert, then post-train on the target platform. At inference, X-World responds at 20 Hz and overlaps updates with execution via asynchronous real-time chunking. X-World performs strongly on LIBERO and under zero-shot shifts on LIBERO-Plus. On hardware, it averages 81.7% success across six bimanual tasks on the side-mounted dual-arm UR5e, one of three real-robot configurations presented.

</details>

![[assets/2611.33911_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33911](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33911)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33911
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/CoTAMP_Cooperative_Task_and_Motion_Planning_for_Quadruped_Robots_with_Large_Language_Models|CoTAMP: Cooperative Task and Motion Planning for Quadruped Robots with Large Language Models]]
- **作者**: Woojin Kim, Jemin Hwangbo
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 3.9（加权：具身智能 1.5，大模型 0.3，机器人 2.1）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《CoTAMP: Cooperative Task and Motion Planning for Quadruped Robots with Large Language Models》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Some environments cannot be traversed by a single quadruped robot, for example a ledge that one robot can climb only from its partner's back or a gap that must be bridged with boxes. Solving them requires cooperative task and motion planning (TAMP). Previous works are limited to the motions of a single robot or only select actions from a predefined set of skills. We propose a framework with two parts: task planning that invents motions not defined in advance, and motion generation that realizes the invented motions. Both parts consist of large language model (LLM)-based modules and code-based modules. Task planning divides the trajectory of the whole task into steps and fixes the robot poses at the step boundaries. Motion generation produces keyframes that describe the motion of each step and turns them into a continuous trajectory through kinematic synthesis. The code-based modules check every plan and motion and return failures to the LLM-based modules for correction. The whole trajectory is then refined by trajectory optimization to satisfy the dynamics. On three simulated courses with two RAIBO2 robot models, our framework produced kinematically valid references in all 30 trials, all of which converged in trajectory optimization. Compared with several baselines, our framework showed advantages in success rate, dynamic feasibility, execution time, and generation time and cost. Project page: https://woojin8098.github.io/CoTAMP/

</details>

![[assets/2611.33865_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33865](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33865)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33865
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Measuring_the_Value_of_One_Shared_Image_at_Robot_Handoffs_in_Vision-and-Language_Navigation|Measuring the Value of One Shared Image at Robot Handoffs in Vision-and-Language Navigation]]
- **作者**: Yuze Lu
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 1.4（加权：具身智能 0.3，机器人 1.1）

#### 研究背景与动机

《Measuring the Value of One Shared Image at Robot Handoffs in Vision-and-Language Navigation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CoNavBench, CommNet, IC3Net, SAGE-Bench, V2VNet, YOLO-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

When the lead robot cannot continue, what should it send the teammate that must finish the route? We replay a frozen receiver at each contact under alternative messages. Across 3,920 autonomous departures in 106 environments, adding the view before the lead's last action block to its executed actions raised success by 7.51 percentage points (pp) per RxR handoff and 8.54 pp per SAGE handoff. Per departure the gains were 2.23 and 1.44 pp, respectively. The lead continuing in simulation defines the empirical full-history ceiling (Solo). At contact, the lead had accumulated a median of 41 frames. Sending one frame when available recovered 24% of the full-history advantage over actions alone in SAGE and 30% in RxR. Over an estimated progress cue in SAGE, the image rescued 100 routes and reversed 54 successes. Near-goal receivers finished outside less often (63.1% to 51.7%, post hoc). In secondary comparisons the earlier view outperformed the contact view by 6-7 pp per handoff. Exact-route specificity and image gains at controlled starts with mostly unknown progress or outside the goal region remained unresolved. Gains were detectable from about 7 kB per handoff. Random transmission matched within size bins nearly equaled the learned sender's team success. At a required handoff, a view of the approach to the last maneuver helps the receiver finish.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Action-conditioned video world models generate future observations from an initial frame and a camera trajectory, but often fail to align camera commands with visual dynamics or represent full 6-DoF scene transformations. We present DuoWM, a first-person drone video world model with two complementary visual conditions. Action flow projects future camera positions and orientations into each target frame, while Object flow forward-warps the initial observation and overlays local 6-DoF transformations to ground the induced scene motion. We further introduce transform-aware pretraining, which pairs object appearance with explicit transformation axes before standard RGB video training. On Sekai-drone dataset, DuoWM achieves the best results across video quality, 3D consistency, and trajectory following. Compared with Captain Safari, the state-of-the-art method, our approach reduces real-domain FVD and LPIPS by 17.2\% and 21.6\%, respectively, while improving game-domain AUC@30 from 0.5003 to 0.5719 and AUC@15 from 0.3088 to 0.3747.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/RoboJackVR_Turning_Robot_Tasks_into_VR_Games_for_Scalable_Demonstration_Collection|RoboJackVR: Turning Robot Tasks into VR Games for Scalable Demonstration Collection]]

![[assets/2611.33731_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33731](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33731)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33731
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/RoboJackVR_Turning_Robot_Tasks_into_VR_Games_for_Scalable_Demonstration_Collection|RoboJackVR: Turning Robot Tasks into VR Games for Scalable Demonstration Collection]]
- **作者**: Ben Ma, Jixuan Cai
- **cs 子类**: cs.HC, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 1.4（加权：具身智能 0.3，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《RoboJackVR: Turning Robot Tasks into VR Games for Scalable Demonstration Collection》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Human-Computer Interaction 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MetaSim, RLBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Scaling robot demonstration datasets requires collection systems that accommodate more participants and diverse tasks, yet teleoperation barriers and task-adaptation effort limit this expansion. We present RoboJackVR, a standalone virtual reality (VR) game in which players manipulate simulated robots to execute combat skills and contribute demonstrations. MuJoCo simulation and demonstration recording run locally on the headset, removing the need for an external PC or simulation server during collection. Tutorials, skill selection, combat consequences, and character progression organize repeated task attempts within a continuing game. Task contracts declare assets, controls, resets, success criteria, and recording semantics; separate game configurations specify guidance, attempt groups, skill effects, and course placement. Shared bindings connect these declarations to task execution, outcome handling, and recording, allowing tasks to reuse the game flow while retaining their control and success semantics. Batch tools package supported resources and generate configurations, with manual adaptation and review for new interfaces and unsupported predicates. We instantiate the approach with SO101-Nexus-derived and LIBERO tasks. On the 66 LIBERO-90 tasks covered by the collected data, ACT policies trained solely on VR demonstrations retain approximately 91% of the full-standard simulation-demonstration baseline's success rate on held-out initial states, averaged over four training seeds. These results support the utility of play-collected VR demonstrations for policy learning in this setting.

</details>

![[assets/2611.33579_first_page.png|800]]

- **作者**: Wongyun Yu, Honggeun Kim, Donguk Lee, Minchan Kim, Kyoung Whan Choe, Minsu Cho
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《DexEngine: Scaling Human Demonstrations into Physically Executable Whole-Robot Data》归入 机器人、具身智能、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DexManipNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A growing body of recent work addresses the retargeting of human hand-object demonstrations to robotic hands. To train whole-robot policies, however, the retargeted motions must be kinematically feasible and physically executable, and be produced efficiently at scale. We present DexEngine, a simulation pipeline that turns human manipulation data into train-ready, physically executable arm-hand trajectories. DexEngine first places each clip's hand-object motion where the arms can reach it, finds an arm motion that follows the human hands, and then corrects the motion in physics simulation with residual reinforcement learning. From nine human hand-object datasets, we generate about 5,200 clips for each of four dexterous hands, augmented into 65,000 trajectories (116 h) and 325,000 episodes (578 h) in total. Even with arms attached, DexEngine tracks objects more accurately than prior floating-hand methods and runs over 10x faster than RL-based ones. Policies trained on our data far outperform those trained on the same clips without physics correction or with the arms attached after a floating-hand correction, showing that fine-tuning data need physics correction. Mid-training on our data further improves them at equal fine-tuning budget, in simulation and on a real robot.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Skeptic_Grounding_Failure_Diagnosis_for_Robot_Skill_Discovery|Skeptic: Grounding Failure Diagnosis for Robot Skill Discovery]]

![[assets/2611.33560_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33560](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33560)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33560
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Skeptic_Grounding_Failure_Diagnosis_for_Robot_Skill_Discovery|Skeptic: Grounding Failure Diagnosis for Robot Skill Discovery]]
- **作者**: Wenfang Sun, Jianghao Deng, Wenzhe Yin, Yingjun Du, Cees G. M. Snoek
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 大模型, 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Skeptic: Grounding Failure Diagnosis for Robot Skill Discovery》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CaP-RL, Contact-GraspNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot agents discover reusable skills by revising programs from execution feedback and retaining experience for later tasks. This process can fail when an agent treats an unsuccessful program as evidence that the task is physically impossible, ending exploration with revision budget remaining. We study this failure in Qwen3.8-27B and examine two decisions that shape skill discovery: I) how execution failures are diagnosed, and II) which lessons enter the shared skill library. Failure diagnosis requires the agent to reconstruct physical action effects from multimodal traces, while independent reproduction of a proposed repair does not require adequate task performance from its source program. We introduce Skeptic, a training-free protocol that uses measured action outcomes to guide repair and measured task success to govern skill admission. A grounded event log reports action effects and object end states from depth images and proprioception, separating sensor observations from inferred diagnoses. Verified promotion adds a development-success requirement on the source program to the existing independent reproduction check. Across 60 audited tasks, the mean number of tasks with false impossibility judgments decreases from 19.5 to 8.5 per campaign. On 80 LIBERO-Pro tasks and seven Robosuite tasks, Skeptic improves held-out success by 14.9 and 11.7 percentage points, with frozen model weights and the same program-revision budget.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Model Predictive Control of a Multirotor UAV with a Cable-Suspended Payload: A Comparison of Shifted and Transformer-Based Warm Starts》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This study investigates model predictive control (MPC) of a multirotor drone (unmanned aerial vehicle, UAV) carrying a cable-suspended payload, comparing conventional shifted initialization with Transformer-based warm starting of a sequential convex programming (SCP) optimizer. The controller uses a coupled mechanical model with an elastic cable and a double-pendulum representation of the suspended load. Its formulation includes move-blocked control inputs, finite-difference sensitivities, constrained quadratic subproblems, and input-feasibility projection. A Transformer trained on expert SCP solutions predicts an initial control sequence, which is subsequently refined by the same optimizer used for the conventional methods. Numerical experiments compare zero, shifted, and Transformer initialization under identical controller settings. For independently solved MPC problems, Transformer initialization reduces computation time relative to zero initialization. In sequential closed-loop simulations, however, shifting the preceding optimized control sequence provides the lowest computation time, while all three methods produce similar tracking behavior. These findings emphasize the importance of comparing learned warm starts with conventional initialization methods that reuse previous MPC solutions. The measured optimization times exceed the control sampling period; consequently, real-time implementation is not demonstrated. Keywords: Model predictive control; sequential convex programming; Transformer; warm start; cable-suspended payload; multirotor drone.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Confidently Lost: Silent Failure and Edge Deployment of Dense Visual SLAM under Photon Starvation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Dense visual SLAM built on learned two-view geometry is evaluated in good light; in low light it fails silently. Two frames of 9-level quantized noise, no scene at all, give MASt3R-SLAM's network a cross-view geometric self-consistency of 0.936; a real dark frame pair gives 0.935. Starved of structure, the network invents the same geometry for both views and the system trusts the agreement: on a dark sequence it consumes 1,605 frames, emits 7 keyframes, rejects no factor-graph edges and reports full frame rate, while over 95% of the correspondences it accepts violate the ground-truth epipolar geometry. One inflated statistic is read by two subsystems against thresholds in opposite directions, so keyframing starves while relocalization accepts almost every candidate. The same statistic, read at keyframe-pair baselines, separates the confident collapse from working runs on the ETH3D dark sequences without ground truth or extra computation (in-sample). Low-light-adapted weights take tracked trajectories from 1/6 to 5/6, as does retuning two thresholds; neither yields accurate ones, because the adaptation withdraws confidence from wrong geometry rather than finding right geometry. Replayed on a Jetson AGX Orin carried by a legged robot, the mechanism's signature reproduces on the two darkest of four routes, and which of its faces appears varies with route geometry and sensor encoding. The network does not lose confidence in the dark; it manufactures it.

</details>
